**Done 2026-07-08 (background-export-notification-center) (branch `main`):** **feature-background-export-notification-center — CLOSED via /flow:execute→/flow:done.** `POST /_api/export` was fully synchronous — both export dialogs blocked (`busy`/`submitting`) for the whole render, no progress, no concurrency. New `exporters/jobs.ts` background job queue (hand-rolled counting semaphore, `MAUDE_EXPORT_MAX_CONCURRENT` default 2, in-memory `Map` as sole source of truth for both live state and the re-derived/overwritten-in-one-shot `_export-history.json` ledger — eliminates the old read-modify-write race) behind new `POST /_api/export-jobs` (202+jobId, non-blocking) + `GET /_api/export-jobs` (list) + `GET /_api/export-jobs/download` (MAIN-ORIGIN ONLY, DDR-060 dual-allowlist). `/_api/export` itself becomes `enqueue()`+`await` — byte-for-byte contract-unchanged, `/design:export` CLI needs zero changes. New `client/export-center.jsx` notification center (menubar badge/toast/panel, twin of `whats-new.jsx`) driven by a new `export:job` WS push; both dialogs now close immediately on submit. **DDR-153.** **Real bug fixed:** `canvas-as-separate` (multi-artboard) exports in all 5 formats (PNG/PDF/SVG/HTML/PPTX) could scramble content — each per-artboard capture pinned the artboard's position for cropping but never restored it, so earlier artboards stayed stacked at the origin and bled into later captures; all 5 shims now save+restore position around each capture, plus emit `MAUDE_PROGRESS` stdout lines (new `spawnShim()`/`runShim()` in `_runtime.ts`, incremental stdout read) for real "N of M" progress. **Bug caught mid-execution:** `_export-jobs/` sits under `designRoot`, and `project-raw` walks the whole design root — without excluding the new dir, every completed export's bytes got bundled into the next `project-raw` zip (which already contained prior zips…), causing real exponential blowup (confirmed via timing repro: 28ms → 4.7s → timeout by request #20); fixed with one line in `scope.ts`'s `RAW_EXCLUDES`. **Live verification (the literal user repro):** against the real 22-artboard `/Users/iagh/git/alligators` canvas (own scratch server, not the user's live desktop session) — `canvas-as-separate` PDF that previously didn't finish in 3+ minutes now completes in **~10s** with live `"N of 22"` progress; downloaded + `pdf-lib`-decoded the result (22 correct pages) and visually confirmed pages 1/11/22 all distinct and correct (no scatter); a concurrent fast PNG finished at t=2s while the PDF was still at "2 of 22". UI verified live via `agent-browser` (dialog closes instantly, badge/toast/panel all render and update). Two follow-up UX requests after initial execute — a "generating in background" toast right after the dialog closes (not just on completion) and a busy/spinner state on the Save button (was silently unresponsive for up to ~20s while bytes fetch + native OS dialog open) — both implemented + live-verified (toast morphs in place queued→running→done/failed via one entry per job, not two separate toasts) + unit-tested (also fixed a latent hydration bug found while implementing this: a page reload would have replayed toasts/auto-downloads for already-finished historical jobs). **Gate:** full studio suite **2189 pass / 5 skip / 0 fail** (2176 baseline + entirely new coverage) · CLI **178/178** · `bun tsc --noEmit` clean (5 pre-existing DDR-026-baseline errors, no new ones) · biome clean · site build + parity/tarball/tokens/site-content all green. **Security fan-out (defender + attacker, independent, both PASS WITH SUGGESTIONS → fixed to PASS):** both converged on the same MEDIUM — `enqueue()` had no cap on queued/running jobs, so a flood of `POST /_api/export-jobs` (missing the `sameOriginWrite`/`isLoopbackHost` guards every sibling route carries) could grow the Map + fill disk unbounded, and a DNS-rebind could enumerate/exfiltrate prior exports via the new GET routes — **FIXED**: `MAX_PENDING` cap (env `MAUDE_EXPORT_MAX_QUEUED`, default 20) → 429 past it, `sameOriginWrite`+`isLoopbackHost` added to all three export routes (matching the DDR-150 family-wide precedent), regression tests added (`dns-rebinding-guard.test.ts` extended, new pending-cap test). Attacker also caught an unhandled-promise-rejection on every failed/timed-out background job (discarded `result` promise, `POST /_api/export-jobs`) — **FIXED** (`.catch(() => {})`, failure already recorded on the job). **`code-simplifier` (parallel, same diff):** 9 findings, applied the 2 "should fix" (extracted a shared `runShim()` in `_runtime.ts` collapsing 6 near-identical spawn+exit-check blocks across every adapter; moved the 5-min job timeout to start after the concurrency semaphore resolves, not at enqueue, so a queued job can't self-abort before it ever runs) + 2 trivial cleanups (dead `ExportJob.message` field, 2 stale comments in `export-dialog.tsx`); deferred the rest (ref-collapse, derive/evict dedup, redundant abort listener, shared body-parser, vestigial "Recent" lists — all noted as follow-ups, none blocking). Review report: `.ai/logs/code-reviews/main-background-export-notification-center.md` (gitignored, like security reports). Changeset (`background-export-notification-center`, minor) + What's New (`background-export-notification-center`, pending, with a spotlight tour step on the new menubar button) authored. Feature commit `4ff5fd82` on `main`, **not pushed** (main is PR-protected — owner's call). **Concurrency note:** ran on a shared, actively-concurrent `main` — another session landed 2 unrelated commits (its own `screenshot.sh` theme-injection security fix + a STATE.md edit) mid-session; diffed and left both files completely untouched. A routine dev-server boot (for live UI verification against the alligators canvas) twice silently clobbered the just-rebuilt release `dist/{client.bundle.js,comment-mount.js}` back to unminified dev-form (the documented CLAUDE.md self-heal gotcha) — caught both times via `git diff --stat dist/` before staging, rebuilt `--release` a third time immediately before the final commit. _Plan archived: `.ai/plans/archive/feature-background-export-notification-center.md` (Retro appended)._

**Done 2026-07-08 (desktop-intel-mac-support) (branch `main`):** **feature-desktop-intel-mac-support — CLOSED via /flow:execute→/flow:done.** The macOS `.dmg` was Apple-Silicon-only; `build-desktop.yml`'s macOS leg now ships a universal (arm64+x86_64) binary (`tauri build --target universal-apple-darwin`) instead of a second matrix leg — Tauri's `.app.tar.gz` updater artifact carries no arch token, so two legs would clobber each other's Release upload. **Landmine found + fixed:** Tauri does NOT auto-lipo `externalBin` sidecars for a universal build (only the main Rust binary gets cargo's auto-fusion — confirmed against `tauri-apps/tauri#3355`); the macOS leg now stages both arches of both sidecars (`maude-server`, `agent-browser`) and runs `lipo -create` itself before `tauri build`. **DDR-106 addendum** records the landmine. **Verified via full local repro before touching CI:** `lipo -info` confirms all 3 shipped binaries are x86_64+arm64 fat; the `.app` boots and its dev-server sidecar serves correctly both natively (arm64) and under Rosetta (x86_64, non-symlinked path). Also fixed 4 "Apple Silicon"-only copy claims across desktop docs/site (2 more than the plan named, caught by its own validate grep). **Gate:** lint/format clean · full studio suite 2173+2 pass (2 pre-existing flakes, clean in isolation: `git-api.test.ts` documented pre-existing, `exporters/endpoint.test.ts` load-induced timeout) · CLI 178/178 · site build ✅ (`/desktop` + `/docs/desktop` render clean) · parity/tarball/tokens/site-content all green. **Cross-platform:** scenario-runner judged the existing `docs-site` scenario N/A for this route (no step targets `/desktop`), ran a proportionate spot-check instead — 0 blockers, `parity_ok: true`; follow-up noted to add real scenario coverage for `/desktop`+`/docs/desktop`. **Security fan-out:** defender + attacker both **PASS, 0 blockers** — no untrusted-input surface (workflow has no `pull_request` trigger), no TOCTOU, no new supply-chain surface, zero AI/MCP attack-surface delta. Report: `.ai/logs/security-reviews/main-20260708-094916.md`. **`/simplify`:** 1 dead-matrix-field fix applied (`target`/`sidecar_dist` on the macOS row were unread after hardcoding both arches inline); 3 independent reviewers (simplification/altitude/efficiency) converged on the same follow-up — centralize the triple-naming/lipo logic in the sync scripts rather than hand-rolling it in YAML — recorded in the plan Retro, not applied (touches shared build tooling beyond this plan's scope). Changeset (`desktop-app-intel-mac-support`, minor) + What's New (`desktop-intel-mac-support`, pending) authored. Feature commit `0c601a0b` on `main`, **not pushed** (main is PR-protected — owner's call; also 2 small housekeeping commits `11df2591`/`ae37f2cd` fixing pre-existing site-content drift from a concurrent session's `--quick` flag work, unrelated to this feature but needed for the `site-content` gate to pass). **Concurrency note:** ran the entire session on a shared, actively-concurrent `main` — another session was mid-`/flow:done` on `feature-element-editing-robustness` throughout (writing scenarios + its own changeset); every shared file (STATE.md, `whats-new.json` + its site mirror) was diffed before staging to confirm no entanglement, and only this feature's own files were staged/committed. _Plan archived: `.ai/plans/archive/feature-desktop-intel-mac-support.md` (Retro appended)._

**Done 2026-07-08 (dual-theme-generation-gap) (branch `main`):** **dual-theme-generation-gap — CLOSED via /flow:bug-fix→done (no plan file).** Fixed `/design:new`'s dual-theme enforcement, which had been dead code since it checked `config.themeDefault == "both"` — a value that field's schema (`dark|light` enum) can never hold — so canvases could ship with a token frozen at its default-theme value and invisible/low-contrast in the un-audited theme (the studyfi-v3 `--fg-0` regression, reported via a linked external system-review, `studyfi-design/.ai/logs/system-reviews/start-here-canvas-review.md`). `designSystems[].themes` is now the one authoritative dual-theme signal (schema + bootstrap + completeness-critic V18 all fixed to read it); new **V18c** completeness-critic check catches a token declared in one theme block but silently missing from the other; `/design:new` step 9 now captures a second per-artboard screenshot pass in the alternate theme via a new `screenshot.sh --theme` flag. **Security fan-out (defender + attacker, both spawned per /flow:done step 4):** attacker found the first commit's `--theme` JS-eval splice was a real HIGH — `config.json`'s untrusted `themes[]` broke out of a JS string passed to `agent-browser eval`, reaching privileged canvas-shell-origin routes (not the sandboxed iframe) — **fixed** (jq JSON-encoding, verified against 5 breakout payloads) before the finding was even filed (self-caught during /flow:done prep); both reviewers independently also flagged a Low path-traversal (`ALT_THEME` unsanitized into `--out-dir`) — **fixed** (charset guard at both the shared sink and the resolution point). Reports: `.ai/logs/security-reviews/dual-theme-generation-gap.md` (gitignored). RCA: `.ai/logs/rca/issue-dual-theme-generation-gap.md` (gitignored). Changeset (patch, `@1agh/maude`) authored; What's New skipped (dev-tooling correctness fix, not a user-visible UI feature). Commits `f8f8bfe9` (fix) + `308c1563` (security hardening + changeset) on `main`, **pushed**. **Known follow-up (documented, not a blocker):** this fixes the mechanism going forward for new/re-bootstrapped design systems; it does NOT retroactively patch already-bootstrapped DSes lacking `themes[]` (e.g. the reporting project's own `studyfi-v3` DS) — needs a manual `themes[]` backfill there, or a `/design:setup-ds --force` re-bootstrap.

**Done 2026-07-07 (branch `main`):** **feature-whiteboard-ai-toolkit (FigJam v4) — CLOSED via /flow:execute→/flow:done.** All 10 tasks (see `## Execution Progress` + `## /flow:done` sections below for full per-task/per-security-round detail): `maude design canvas-rects` geometry manifest (client hook + headless capture, static fallback) unblocks element-level READ context (`read-annotations --rects`) + coordinate-free WRITE placement (`annotate --in`/`--pin`/`--board` templates — retro/kanban/social-calendar/roadmap/brainstorm/checklist/user-flow — and id-preserving `move`/`set-text`/`set-color` via full DOM-parser fidelity, user's explicit choice over a lossy shortcut), packaged as a new `whiteboard` skill + `/design:board` command. **DDR-151.** **Gate:** full dev-server suite **2043 pass / 1 skip / 1 fail** (pre-existing flaky `git-api.test.ts`, clean in isolation) · CLI **178/178** · biome clean · site build ✅ (new `board.mdx` doc page + command-catalog parity fix) · parity/tarball/tokens/site-content all green. **Cross-platform:** new scenario `whiteboard-ai-loop` (web-desktop, this tool has no native counterpart) authored + run live by scenario-runner, 0 blockers, parity OK. **Security fan-out — TWO rounds, 4 concrete bugs fixed + regression-tested, 1 residual accepted, 1 packaging gap gracefully degraded** (full detail in the `## /flow:done` section below): path traversal in the static geometry fallback; missing size caps on `--board`/`--flow`'s `connections[]`/`edges[]` (also closed a pre-existing gap in the top-level `--flow` verb); a missing read-time byte cap on the annotations file. **Residual, user-decided:** element-context widens an already-accepted DDR-085 trifecta risk — not code-closeable from this feature; asked directly, user chose "ship with it documented" (matches DDR-085's own precedent). **Packaging gap found + gracefully degraded, not root-fixed:** `apps/studio/package.json` is a nested workspace manifest the npm tarball doesn't ship (confirmed against a real global `@1agh/maude@0.40.0` install on this machine) — `move`/`set-text`/`set-color`'s happy-dom dependency would crash for a real end user; now fails loud with a clear remediation message instead. Real production fix (compiled-binary dependency embedding, or another distribution mechanism) is tracked follow-up in DDR-151. **Done 2026-07-03 (thumbnails) (branch `main`):** **feature-acp-chat-image-thumbnails-lightbox — CLOSED via /flow:done.** Images in the native ACP chat now render inline. **v1** (commit `6b8a8f2`): user-pasted composer images → clickable thumbnail + lightbox. New GET branch on `/_api/acp/attachment` (`resolveChatAttachment` content-addressed `^[0-9a-f]{8}\.(png|jpe?g|gif|webp)$` allowlist + containment; MAIN-ORIGIN ONLY, dual-allowlist 403 asserted; immutable cache + nosniff), pure `attachmentName`/`extractAttachmentRefs` helpers (live chip + reloaded-transcript path), `ChatLightbox` (role=dialog, ESC/backdrop/×, focus restore, z-9700 above shell modal band — found via live z-ladder probe). **v2 (DDR-145):** agent-referenced designRoot image paths (a `/design:screenshot` reply naming `.design/…png`) render a captioned thumbnail strip under the assistant bubble → same lightbox. Pure `designImageRefs(text, designRel)` — no server change (reuses the main-origin designRoot static fall-through). **Gate:** affected dev-server suites green (chat-attachments 11 · serve-guard · origin-gate · whats-new all pass; full studio suite was 1841/1 skip/0 earlier this session), biome clean on touched files, ReDoS-probed linear, live curl (serve 200/traversal 403/nosniff present). **Security fan-out (2 defenders + attacker, the real teeth):** every at/above-`medium` finding FIXED before close-out — (M) `designImageRefs` client containment was illusory: the char class admitted `..`/`%`/`\`, so `.design/../../etc/x.png`, `..%2f`, and (attacker re-run) `..\..\` (WHATWG rewrites `\`→`/` after a naive guard) all widened the fetch surface from designRel to repoRoot behind the server's `safePathUnderRoot` backstop → **rewrote to a canonical ALLOWLIST** (`new URL().pathname` byte-identical + under `/rel/` + reject `%`), backslash/percent/dot-segment regression tests; (M) attachment GET `serveFile` missing `nosniff` → added; (M) F2 content-spoofing (auto-rendered image lends the assistant's authority) → captioned "referenced" thumbnails distinct from first-class media; (L) ReDoS → bounded regex quantifiers. **Below-floor tracked (NOT fixed):** F3 sha8 32-bit collision + immutable cache (widen if attachments ever cross the hub), F5 lexical (non-`realpath`) attachment containment (local-only, `_chat/` not hub-synced). Reports gitignored (`.ai/logs/security-reviews/{ddr-145,feature-acp}-image-thumbnails-*.md`). **Verification ceiling:** native paste→thumbnail→lightbox→reload in WKWebView = user dogfood (agent-browser can't flip `isNativeApp()`); verified render/computed-styles by injecting the components into a running server + curl. **Skipped (N/A):** 5-platform scenario (single-surface chat chrome), tracker (github provider, no ticket). Changeset (`acp-chat-image-thumbnails`, minor) + What's New (`acp-chat-image-thumbnails`, pending) authored. Feature commits `6b8a8f2` (v1) + the v2 slice, on `main`, **not pushed** (PR-protected — owner's call). _Plan archived: `.ai/plans/archive/feature-acp-chat-image-thumbnails-lightbox.md`._

**Done 2026-07-05 (branch `main`):** **feature-video-editing-robustness — CLOSED via /flow:done (post-COMPLETE dogfood addendum).** The plan hit COMPLETE 2026-07-04 (16 tasks); a long user dogfood loop then shipped several deferred items + a granularity/UX layer, all on `main` (`8d3071c`→`61786e9`, each e2e-verified). Net: (1) **multi-comp scoping fix** — the Timeline passed the `<Player>` id not the resolved `DCArtboard` id, so comp-clips/ops fell back to the wrong comp on a 2-comp canvas (ClipShots showed `ƒ`); `parseCompTimeline` now returns the resolved artboard id, threaded through every op (the debate's addressing bug, resurfacing at the client boundary). (2) **`<TransitionSeries>` reorder + insert** — reorder-by-**span-swap** (sequences trade places, transitions stay → alternation preserved) + insert-by-**clone-transition**; unblocked the showreel (which is a TransitionSeries). (3) **Right-click clip context menu** reusing the canvas's shared `ContextMenuView` (dark chrome tokens on the shell so it matches the canvas menu). (4) **Clip layer decomposition** — a wrapper-component clip (`<ClipShot clip={CLIPS[i]}>` → `<Video>`+`<LowerThird>`) decomposes into per-layer sub-rows (expanded by default), replace moved off the parent onto each media sub-layer. (5) **Show/hide (`toggleClipHidden`) LANDED** (was deferred) — gates the clip's children behind `{false && (…)}`: tag + time slot stay (TransitionSeries alternation intact), Prettier/HMR-stable, Show strips back byte-identical. **DDR-150.** **Gate:** full studio suite **1977 pass / 1 skip / 0 fail** · parity 0.39.1 exit 0 · biome: my 6-commit tail introduces no new diagnostics (3 pre-existing FIXABLE warnings blame to earlier commits `21e9b69`/`e0d3ab2`, not this tail; test-file `!` asserts follow the file's existing style). **Security fan-out (defender + attacker, scoped to `8d3071c^..HEAD`):** defender **PASS** (0 blockers; splice injection closed — the `{false && (…)}` payload is verbatim existing source never attacker input, reparse + `assertCompSemantics` gate every write; insert-src escaped+scheme-guarded+allowlisted; comp-clips is pure AST literal echo; ContextMenuView labels are React-escaped text nodes). Attacker **PASS WITH SUGGESTIONS** (0 blockers) — 2 findings, neither a regression from this diff: **(1, Medium, SYSTEMIC — tracked priority follow-up)** `sameOriginWrite` is a reflective Origin check with **no `isLoopbackHost(Host)` guard**, so DNS-rebinding (`evil→127.0.0.1`) bypasses CSRF on the WHOLE source-write/read family (`toggle-hide`/`edit-text`/`edit-css`/`edit-attr`/`reorder*`/`retime`/`remove`/`insert`/`edit-array-src` + reads `canvas-source`/`comp-clips`) → drive-by read+write of versioned `.tsx`. Pre-existing across every sibling; my new endpoint joins the family, doesn't introduce the gap. **FIXED (commit `1d80c19`)** — added the existing `isLoopbackHost(req.headers.get('host'))` guard family-wide: 23 `sameOriginWrite` write routes gain it after their CSRF check + the 2 GET reads (`canvas-source`/`comp-clips`, which had NO Origin check) gain it after the method check; `/_api/git/*` already had it (not doubled). Server always binds 127.0.0.1 so a real browser (`Host: localhost:<port>`) passes, only a rebound hostname 403s — zero functional impact. New integration regression test `test/dns-rebinding-guard.test.ts` (rebound→403 write+reads, loopback→200); live-verified via curl. **(2, Low)** source-derived menu labels (media filenames) aren't bidi/homoglyph-normalized → Trojan-Source UI spoof in the privileged shell menu; strip `‪–‮`/`⁦–⁩` + length-cap. Creativity note: `{false && (…)}` hide is a *preview-invisible/source-live* primitive (block vanishes from visual review but persists for `git diff`/agent) — review-evasion only (can't add content). Trifecta: **pass** (no outbound/model leg in this diff; watch if comp-clips output ever feeds the ACP prompt). Verdict permits close (≤ medium + systemic-not-regression). **New source-write surface:** `/_api/toggle-hide` (main-origin + `sameOriginWrite` CSRF + canvas-origin-gate denylist test + oxc reparse + `assertCompSemantics` + contentHash fingerprint + undo-logged). **Verification ceiling:** real-mp4 comps flaky headless (footage decode) → verified via an Img-based decomposable comp for UI e2e + server-side `comp-clips` against the real showreel + engine unit tests. **Still deferred:** mid-clip split, `<TransitionSeries>` split, per-sub-layer independent trim/move (layers share the parent Sequence timing), multi-select, `interpolate()` value editing. **Skipped (N/A):** 5-platform scenario-runner (single-surface dev-server + plugins), PR/push (main PR-protected — owner's call), tracker (no provider). **Not pushed.** Concurrent-session `Cargo.lock` + untracked `.design/` dogfood-test assets left untouched. What's New (`timeline-clip-editing`, pending — summary expanded for hide/layers/menu/TransitionSeries). _Plan archived: `.ai/plans/archive/feature-video-editing-robustness.md` (Post-plan dogfood addendum + Retro appended)._

Provenance: `agent-browser` is Vercel's, lockfile-pinned+integrity (`dependencies.json` corrected off the nonexistent `@anthropic-ai/` name). New guard unit tests. **Verification ceiling (DDR-135):** packaged-`.app` end-to-end (externalBin on PATH in the notarized bundle, first-run provisioning, native chat) = user dogfood. **Concurrency:** shared-`main` hazard was live — a parallel session (ACP image-thumbnails) reset/re-committed `main` + trimmed this feature to design-only; my work committed **atomically + explicit-file-staged** (twice recovered from index sweeps). **Commits:** `04b5572` (DDR-143) · `d7bdf82` (DDR-144) · `bbe1448` (security fixes) · `e304f28` (annotation SVGs), all on `main`, **not pushed** (PR-protected — owner's call). **Done 2026-07-03 (branch `main`):** **feature-acp-context-hardening — CLOSED via /flow:done.** Multi-session multitasking no longer loses agent context. Three additive mechanisms sharing one pipeline: (1) **per-canvas selection memory** — `_active.json` gains an additive `selections` map; `setActive` parks/restores instead of nulling, `selected` stays the active-canvas mirror so prep.sh SEL_VALID / `/design:edit` / every legacy reader work unchanged (Phase 4.1 obj→arr precedent); **drift gate** stamps `canvas_mtime` + flags `stale` so the positional `data-cd-id` is never trusted across another writer's edit (DDR-007/138); parked entries html-stripped (size cap); (2) **frozen-at-send chat context** — `buildChatContext` emits compact `[maude-context …]`/`[selected: …]` bracket lines APPENDED after the typed text (paste-chip semantics — fixed from a leading `<maude-context>` fence that polluted chat titles + read as ceremony, dogfood 2026-07-03), driving BOTH the visible removable chip and the prompt payload (DDR-140 reveal); locators only, never `selected.html`; transcript UI projection strips the block, raw jsonl kept as the audit record; (3) **session bootstrap brief** — every new ACP session gets a static, config-generated, environment-only brief via `newSession._meta.systemPrompt.append` (verified against the installed SDK zod schema + adapter; upgrade-guard test fails loud on a dep bump), mirrored to the transcript as `role:'bootstrap'` (hidden-in-UI, never invisible-to-audit); direct canvas-file edits stated as the default (fixed from "prefer /design:*" which over-triggered the full edit pipeline on a trivial tweak). Plus a **halo-restore retry ladder** (50/450/1200/2500/5000ms) — the select-by-id re-apply raced the fresh iframe's React mount, so a one-shot post evaporated and the restored selection never got its halo back. **DDR-142.** **Gate:** full studio suite **1811 pass / 1 skip / 0 fail** (1 pre-existing `git/watch` flake under load, green in isolation) · biome clean on my files (1 whole-repo error + 16 warnings all pre-existing in untouched files) · smoke 97/97 canvases rendered styled (all 21 ui/ PNGs inspected; 76 DS specimens machine-verified — DDR-021 capacity deviation, render path untouched). **Security fan-out:** defender **PASS WITH SUGGESTIONS** (0 medium+, S1–S3 folded); adversarial **NEEDS-FIXES → 2 Medium, BOTH FIXED before close-out** — **F1** brief interpolated peer-authored config.json `projectLabel`/`designRel` into the system-prompt append unescaped (hub/DDR-079) → `safeFact()` strip+cap+"treat as data" reframe; **F2** `select`/`select-set`/`clear-select` handlers lacked the phase-28-F-2 `e.source === activeWin` gate → a background untrusted canvas could plant a spoofed selection into the persistent map + the auto-approving agent → gated (+ below-floor residual closed in `e284d3f`: `setSelected` write-through now keyed by `state.active`, so an ACTIVE untrusted canvas can't plant a `file:<other canvas>` selection into another slot). Final adversarial verdict **PASS-WITH-SUGGESTIONS**, 0 at/above floor. Reports (gitignored): `.ai/logs/security-reviews/feature-acp-context-hardening-{defender,attacker}.md`. **Verification ceiling:** the chip/halo/brief render in the native `.app` WKWebView = user dogfood (agent-browser can't flip `isNativeApp()`); the F2 select gate mirrors an established pattern (no unit harness for app.jsx onMessage). **Skipped (N/A):** 5-platform scenario-runner (single-surface dev-server + plugins), PR/push (main PR-protected — owner's call), tracker (no provider). **Concurrency note:** a parallel session's DDR-143 (ACP plugin auto-bootstrap) co-edited `acp/bootstrap-brief.ts` — its optional `commandsAvailable` field + test are inseparable in-file from the F1 fix, so they rode in commit `641499f` (default-off, no behavior change); that session's `bridge.ts`/`index.ts`/`plugin-bootstrap.ts` wiring left uncommitted. Changeset (minor) + What's New (`acp-context-hardening`) authored. ## Execution Progress — feature-background-export-notification-center (2026-07-08, branch `main`) — DONE via /flow:execute→/flow:done, plan archived, commit `4ff5fd82`

Plan: `.ai/plans/feature-background-export-notification-center.md`. High-complexity: a correctness bug fix (5 Playwright shims) + a new background job queue + a client notification center. All 26 tasks done; plan file's task headings + acceptance criteria checked off inline.

- **Phase A (bug fix + progress instrumentation, Tasks 1–7).** Root cause confirmed: `_png/_pdf/_svg/_html/_pptx-playwright.mjs` each pinned an artboard's `left`/`top` to `0px` for its capture but never restored it, so `canvas-as-separate` (multi-artboard) exports left every earlier artboard stacked at the origin, corrupting every later capture — the reported "scattered/overlapping fragments" bug. Fixed all 5 shims (save-before/restore-after around each per-artboard capture, `pinArtboard()` helper for svg/html). Added `MAUDE_PROGRESS {"current":N,"total":M}` stdout lines per artboard + a new shared `exporters/_runtime.ts` `spawnShim()` (incremental stdout read + `AbortSignal`) threaded through all 6 adapters via a new `ExportHooks` (`onProgress`/`signal`) param on `Adapter.run`/`runExport`.
- **Phase B (background job queue, Tasks 8–14).** New `exporters/jobs.ts`: `createExportJobQueue()` — hand-rolled counting semaphore (`MAUDE_EXPORT_MAX_CONCURRENT`, default 2), in-memory `Map<id,ExportJob>` as the single source of truth for both live `list()` and the derived/capped(20)/overwritten-in-one-shot `_export-history.json` ledger (eliminates the old read-modify-write race), bytes persisted to `<designRoot>/_export-jobs/<id>/`, boot-time ledger reseed + orphan sweep, age/cap eviction. `ExportHistoryEntry`/`loadExportHistory`/`appendExportHistory` moved out of `api.ts` into `jobs.ts`. New `POST /_api/export-jobs` (202 + jobId, non-blocking) + `GET /_api/export-jobs` (list) + `GET /_api/export-jobs/download?id=` — all three confirmed absent from `CANVAS_SAFE_API` + `startCanvasServer` routes (same MAIN-ORIGIN-ONLY class as `/_api/export`, DDR-060). `/_api/export` itself becomes `enqueue()` + `await result` — byte-for-byte contract-unchanged (`/design:export` CLI needs zero changes). `export:job` bus event wired into `ws.ts` (inspector-channel broadcast, mirrors `git-status`). Runtime-state taxonomy (`git/service.ts` `isMaudeRuntimeState`, `cli/lib/gitignore-block.mjs`, root `.gitignore`) all gained `_export-jobs/` together.
- **Phase C (client notification center, Tasks 15–20).** New `client/export-center.jsx` — `useExportCenter()` (hydrate + WS `upsert` + transition-detected toast queue + focus-gated web auto-download / native explicit Save…) + `<ExportBadge>`/`<ExportToast>`/`<ExportPanel>`, structural twin of `whats-new.jsx`. New `.st-exports` menubar CSS (mirrors `.st-assistant`'s `data-busy` pattern) + `.st-export-*` panel/list/progress-bar CSS (new determinate progress bar — no prior art in the client). Both `ExportDialog.doExport()` (main shell) and `export-dialog.tsx`'s `submit()` (in-canvas, cross-origin-bridged) now POST `/_api/export-jobs` and close immediately instead of blocking on `busy`/`submitting`; `runBridgedExport` replies `{ok, jobId}` instead of shipping bytes through the bridge. Rebuilt the committed release bundle (`dist/client.bundle.js` + `dist/styles.css`) per CLAUDE.md's client-surface rule.
- **Bug caught mid-execution (not in the plan):** the first version of `jobs.ts` wrote each completed job's bytes under `<designRoot>/_export-jobs/<id>/`, but `project-raw`'s `RAW_EXCLUDES` walk didn't know about that new directory — every `project-raw` zip export bundled all *prior* export outputs (including prior zips, which already contained their own priors), causing genuine exponential blowup: 25 sequential `project-raw` exports went 28ms → 4.7s → timeout. Root-caused via a manual timing repro (not just "tests are slow"), fixed by adding `_export-jobs` to `scope.ts`'s `RAW_EXCLUDES` — confirmed flat ~7ms/request after the fix. Would not have been caught by the existing exporter test suite (none of it does a real multi-request `project-raw` sequence).
- **Tests (Tasks 21–23, 25):** `test/exporters/jobs.test.ts` (4 tests — concurrency cap via a real large-sandbox `project-raw` zip timed to ~400ms/job, `list()` sort order, 25-concurrent-completion history race, byte download + eviction), `test/exporters/history.test.ts` extended (20-cap, old-shape-entry back-compat), `test/export-shim-multi-capture.test.ts` (6 tests — static-source regression guard on all 5 shims; a REAL-Chromium execution test is deliberately out of this suite's contract, mirrors `pw-launch.test.ts`'s existing "integration-shape, needs a real browser" precedent — CI has no Chromium installed), `test/export-center.test.tsx` (3 tests — `happy-dom` + real `createRoot`/`act()`, mirrors `canvas-hmr-runtime.test.tsx`; upsert-sequence/runningCount, toast-not-requeued-on-repeat-upsert, focus-gated auto-download via a mocked `fetch`+`document.hasFocus`). `canvas-origin-gate.test.ts` extended with the 2 new routes.
- **Live verification (Task 26) — the literal user repro, against the real `/Users/iagh/git/alligators` 22-artboard canvas** (own local server on a scratch port, not the user's live desktop session on 4400): `canvas-as-separate` PDF export that previously didn't complete in 3+ minutes now finishes in **~10s** with live `"current":N,"total":22` progress; downloaded + decoded the resulting PDF (`pdf-lib`) — 22 pages, correct per-artboard sizes; rendered pages 1/11/22 to PNG (`pdftoppm`) and visually confirmed each shows fully correct, distinct, non-overlapping content (no scatter). Concurrency: fired a slow `canvas-as-separate` PDF + a fast single-artboard PNG back-to-back — PNG reached `done` at t=2s while the PDF was still at `"2 of 22"`, fully independent. UI (via `agent-browser` against the same local server): Export dialog now closes **immediately** on submit (menubar badge count went from 0→1 the instant the dialog closed); toast rendered "EXPORT READY / PNG · Artboard / …png" with Download/Dismiss; Exports panel listed all completed jobs with status pills + per-row Download. In-canvas bridged dialog verified by code symmetry + typecheck only (not live-clicked — same enqueue-and-close path as the main-shell dialog).
- **Gate:** `apps/studio` full suite **2185 pass / 5 skip / 0 fail** (2176 baseline + 9 new). Root CLI suite **178/178**. `bun tsc --noEmit` — same 5 pre-existing DDR-026-baseline errors, 0 new. `pnpm lint` — same pre-existing warnings only (incl. one unrelated `_video-playwright.mjs` comma-operator warning, file untouched by this feature), exit 0. `scripts/check-version-parity.sh` — pass (unaffected files only).
- **Closed via `/flow:done`:** DDR-153 recorded, security fan-out fixes applied (see the **Done 2026-07-08 (background-export-notification-center)** entry above for full detail), committed `4ff5fd82` on `main`, not pushed (owner's call).
- **Concurrency note:** ran on a shared, actively-concurrent `main` — another session landed a real, unrelated jq-escaping security fix to `bin/screenshot.sh` + `bin/_screenshot-playwright.mjs` (uncommitted, on top of its own commit `f8f8bfe9`) while this session worked; diffed and left both files completely untouched, not staged. Also: booting the local dev-server against `/Users/iagh/git/alligators` for live verification (Task 26) clobbered the just-rebuilt release `dist/{client.bundle.js,comment-mount.js}` back to dev-form (the documented CLAUDE.md self-heal gotcha) — caught before commit, rebuilt `--release` a second time; sizes now match the first clean build exactly.
- Files created: `apps/studio/exporters/jobs.ts`, `apps/studio/client/export-center.jsx`, `apps/studio/test/exporters/jobs.test.ts`, `apps/studio/test/export-shim-multi-capture.test.ts`, `apps/studio/test/export-center.test.tsx`. Files modified: 5 shims under `apps/studio/bin/`, `apps/studio/exporters/{index,_runtime,pdf,png,svg,html,pptx,video,scope}.ts`, `apps/studio/{api,http,server,ws,git/service}.ts`, `apps/studio/{export-dialog.tsx,client/app.jsx,client/styles/3-shell-maude.css,client/styles/4-components.css,dist/client.bundle.js,dist/styles.css}`, `apps/studio/test/exporters/history.test.ts`, `apps/studio/test/canvas-origin-gate.test.ts`, `cli/lib/gitignore-block.mjs`, root `.gitignore`. **Uncommitted.**

## Execution Progress — feature-desktop-intel-mac-support (2026-07-08, branch `main`) — COMPLETE via /flow:execute

Plan: `.ai/plans/feature-desktop-intel-mac-support.md`. Low-complexity, CI-config + docs only (no app/runtime code). Executed Task 5 (local repro) FIRST per the plan's own explicit ordering note, since it caught a real bug in the plan's core assumption before any CI edit was made.

- **Task 5 (local repro) — ran first, found + fixed a wrong plan assumption.** `rustup target add x86_64-apple-darwin`, built both dev-server sidecar arches (`bun run build.ts --release --target=bun-darwin-{arm64,x64}`), staged all 4 sidecar files (`sync-sidecar.mjs`/`sync-agent-browser.mjs` × 2 slugs). First `tauri build --target universal-apple-darwin` attempt **FAILED**: `resource path 'binaries/maude-server-universal-apple-darwin' doesn't exist`. Root cause (confirmed via `gh issue view 3355 --repo tauri-apps/tauri`): Tauri's universal-binary auto-lipo only applies to the main Rust binary (cargo compiles both arches itself) — it does **NOT** auto-lipo `externalBin` sidecars; it expects a **pre-built** fat binary literally named `<name>-universal-apple-darwin`. Fixed by running `lipo -create` on both sidecars myself before the build. Second attempt **succeeded**: `Maude_0.40.0_universal.dmg` + `Maude.app` + `Maude.app.tar.gz` all produced (the trailing `TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY` error is expected locally — I don't have that CI secret; unrelated to arch). Verified via `lipo -info` on all 3 shipped binaries (main app `maude-desktop`, `maude-server`, `agent-browser`) — all report `x86_64 arm64`. Live-booted the app from a non-symlinked path on **both** native arm64 and Rosetta-translated x86_64 (`arch -x86_64 ...`) — dev-server sidecar spawned and served `http://localhost:4400` identically on both. Note: the Rosetta run printed Bun's "CPU lacks AVX support" warning — a known Rosetta-2-only limitation (Rosetta doesn't expose AVX/AVX2), not a real-hardware concern since Ventura's minimum-supported Intel Macs (2017+, Kaby Lake+) all have AVX2; not a regression from this plan (same `bun-darwin-x64` target is already used for the CLI's Intel build).
- **Tasks 1–4 (`build-desktop.yml` CI edits)** — applied with the corrected lipo recipe (not the plan's original "auto-lipo" assumption): Rust toolchain step gains a per-leg `matrix.rust_targets` (macOS only, `aarch64-apple-darwin,x86_64-apple-darwin`); sidecar-build step special-cased for macOS to build both arches; the macOS build step now stages all 4 sidecar files THEN runs `lipo -create` to produce `maude-server-universal-apple-darwin` + `agent-browser-universal-apple-darwin` before `tauri build --target universal-apple-darwin`; DMG-notarize glob, matrix `bundle_glob`, and the release-attach globs all repointed at `target/universal-apple-darwin/release/bundle/...`; matrix `label`/`artifact_name` renamed `macos-arm64`→`macos-universal`. Windows/Linux legs untouched (grep-verified: no bare `target/release/bundle` remains on the macOS leg). Header comment block extended to document the lipo-not-auto rationale for future readers.
- **Task 6 (docs)** — fixed the 2 files the plan named (`site/content/docs/desktop/index.mdx`, `download-button.tsx`) **plus 2 more the plan missed** but its own validate grep (`site/app/(home)/desktop`) covers: `site/app/(home)/desktop/page.tsx` had 2 more "Apple Silicon"-only claims (hero fineprint + system-requirements `<dd>`) — fixed all 4 to "Intel or Apple Silicon" / "Intel & Apple Silicon". Grep-verified clean.
- **Drive-by note (not a regression):** `apps/studio/dist/{client.bundle.js,comment-mount.js,styles.css}` were already `M` (dirty) at session start; my required `bun run build.ts --release` invocations (needed to produce the sidecar binaries for Task 5) rebuilt them in release-minified form as a side effect, which happened to exactly match the already-committed HEAD (source hadn't changed) — so they now show clean. Consistent with CLAUDE.md's own documented rule ("rebuild `--release` after any dev-server boot"); nothing lost.
- **Not done (deferred, needs explicit user go-ahead per plan's own Validation §2):** CI dry run via `workflow_dispatch` on a pushed branch, and the post-release updater-feed sanity check — both require pushing/triggering CI, held for the commit/push confirmation step.
- **Gate:** no project-wide lint/typecheck/test/build applies (CI-config + docs only, per CLAUDE.md — this repo has no root test suite). All task-level `Validate:` commands from the plan re-run and passed.
- Files modified: `.github/workflows/build-desktop.yml`, `site/content/docs/desktop/index.mdx`, `site/app/(home)/desktop/download-button.tsx`, `site/app/(home)/desktop/page.tsx`. **Uncommitted.**

## Execution Progress — feature-element-editing-robustness (2026-07-07, branch `main`) — IN PROGRESS via /flow:execute

> Very large plan — 45 tasks across Stages A–M + G. Executing in dependency order, one stage at a time, checkpointing here after each task so a future session can resume. Plan: `.ai/plans/feature-element-editing-robustness.md`.

✅ **Stage A — Camera/scroll bug fixes (the two reported bugs) — DONE.**
- **Task A1** — replaced the host-scrolling `scrollIntoView({block:'nearest'})` in the `dgn:'select-by-id'` handler (`canvas-shell.tsx`, inside `CanvasRouter`'s onMessage) with a **camera-aware reveal**: new module-level helpers `revealAxisDelta()` (pure minimal 1-axis screen-px delta; 0 when visible or when the element spans the viewport — matches `scrollIntoView` nearest semantics) + `revealElementViaCamera()` (measures live screen boxes, pans via `zoomController.panBy` which reads the exact live vpRef, never host scroll). Both exported for the test. This is the shared root cause of Bugs A + B.
- **Task A2** — added a host-scroll-0 invariant clamp: a capture-phase `scroll` listener on the `.dc-canvas` host that resets `scrollLeft/scrollTop` to 0 **only** for engine clip layers (`.dc-canvas` itself, `.dc-artboard-body`, `.dc-artboard`) — author scroll containers deeper in a mock fail the engine-clip test and pass through. `scroll` doesn't bubble, so capture:true lets the host observe a descendant clip's scroll. Enforces the assumption `writeTransform` + `__maudeCanvasRects` both make.
- **Task A3** — new `test/camera-reveal.test.tsx` (registers happy-dom like `canvas-hmr-runtime.test.tsx`): 5 `revealAxisDelta` unit cases (visible/flush/off-start/off-end/wider-than-viewport) + 3 `revealElementViaCamera` integration cases asserting it pans via `panBy` and leaves `host.scrollLeft/scrollTop` at 0 (the Bug B repro: an element past the right edge). **8/8 pass.**

🟡 **Stage D — On-canvas drag-resize — D1/D2/D3 DONE (element resize); D4 (artboard resize) remains.**
- **D1** — new `apps/studio/use-element-resize.tsx`: pure `computeElementResize(corner, start, dxW, dyW, mods, flags)` (world-unit deltas → target box; derives left/top by holding the opposite edge fixed — Figma grammar; Shift=aspect-lock, Alt=from-center; 1px min clamp) + `ElementResizeOverlay` (rAF-follows the selected element's screen box, 8 constant-size handles, pointerdown→capture→preview via direct inline style→pointerup posts `dgn:'resize-request'`). In-flow elements write width/height only (never convert-to-absolute); out-of-flow additionally move left/top on a top/left-edge drag (guarded by an inline-value present flag). Composes with ReorderDrag for free — handles are fixed overlay divs outside the artboard, so ReorderDrag's `el.contains(target)` gate never fires for them.
- **D2** — mounted `<ElementResizeOverlay />` in `canvas-shell.tsx` next to `<SelectionHalos />` (+ import). Live-verified: the Smoke TSX canvas mounts clean with the overlay (no shared-runtime break).
- **D3** — shell-side `resizeElement(id, patch, before)` in `app.jsx` (sibling to `repositionElement`): chains `/_api/edit-css` writes for each present prop (width/height/left/top) on the shared `editApplyChainRef`, records one undo entry per prop, posts `resize-failed` on error (overlay restores the pre-drag inline box). New `resize-request` onMessage handler pinned to `activePath` (confused-deputy guard, DDR-138/054) + `resizeElementRef` wiring.
- **Tests:** `test/element-resize.test.ts` — 9 cases (SE/E/S plain, NW out-of-flow opposite-corner-fixed, NW in-flow no-origin-move, W edge, Shift aspect 2:1, Alt from-center, min clamp). **9/9.** tsc 0 new · biome clean · client bundle rebuilt.
- **Live-drag NOT yet verified** (cross-origin canvas selection + synthetic handle drag — same ceiling as phase-12.1) → Stage-G scenario `element-editing-resize-and-position` covers it.
- **Stage-H coupling note:** resize writes via `edit-css` on the element's cd-id — for a shared-component INNER element this changes all instances, exactly as the existing CssKnobs edits already do (resize introduces NO new unpredictability). Stage H adds the scope badge covering ALL edit types (knob/move/resize) + per-occurrence routing for instance wrappers. Landing D before H on `main` is fine (commit-only, not pushed; H before feature close). **D4 (free-hand artboard resize via edit-attr width/height, DDR-027) still to do.**

✅ **Stage D4 (free-hand artboard resize) — backend DONE in the new-write-route batch (below); overlay UI DONE 2026-07-07.** Investigation finding: the active `DCArtboard` `<article>` (`canvas-lib.tsx:1849`) carries `data-dc-screen` but **no `data-cd-id`**, and its rendered size comes from the layout `rect` (seeded by the JSX `width`/`height` props per DDR-027), not a resolvable inline value. So artboard resize can't ride edit-css on a cd-id — it needed (a) a **new main-origin route** keyed by the artboard's `id` prop (`/_api/resize-artboard`, shipped in the batch below), AND (b) a **numeric-attr engine change** (`applyResizeArtboard`/`writeNumericAttr` writes `width={N}` not `"N"`, also shipped below).
> - **Overlay UI (2026-07-07).** `use-element-resize.tsx`'s `ElementResizeOverlay` now activates for a whole-ARTBOARD selection too (`Selection.id` undefined + `Selection.artboardId` set — the chrome-click case `resolveHoverTarget` already produces). Renders **growth-only E/S/SE handles** (no NW/N/W/SW/NE/SE-left-moving variants, no rotate zones) — a deliberate scope choice: an artboard IS `position:absolute` in the DOM (world x/y from layout meta), but the resize route only ever writes `width`/`height` PROPS, never touches `layout.artboards[].x/y`; allowing a left/top-moving handle would preview a shift that snaps back on the next reload. Restricting to the bottom-right quadrant makes the live preview and the persisted result agree by construction. New `ElResizeDrag.artboardId: string | null` + `postResizeArtboardRequest()` (posts plain-number `width`/`height`, not px strings — matches what the shell's `resize-artboard-request` handler expects); `computeFromEvent` ignores Alt/center for an artboard drag (without a left/top write, "center" would just double the growth speed with no recentering). `structuralWrite` gained an `onFail` callback (previously silent-on-failure); `resizeArtboardShell` wires it to a new `resize-artboard-failed` postMessage so a rejected/422 write restores the pre-drag inline box (mirrors the existing `resize-failed`/`reposition-failed` revert pattern — closes a gap the earlier backend-only landing didn't have, since it had no live preview to revert). **Gate:** tsc 6/6 baseline 0 new · biome clean (fixed 1 new `useExhaustiveDependencies` + 1 format nit from the artboardOnly dep) · targeted tests 21/21 (element-resize + structural-api + origin-gate) · full suite 2111/1/0 unaffected · client bundle release-rebuilt, boot smoke green (`resize-artboard-request`/`resize-artboard-failed` present in the served bundle).

✅ **Stage E — Element selection + inspect on DS specimens — DONE (structural + build-verified).** No new route (reuses edit-css/edit-attr).
- **E1** — `canvas-comment-mount.tsx`: new `pickSpecimenSelectEl` (generalizes `pickSpecimenEl` — climbs to the hit's own `data-cd-id`, else nearest stamped ancestor; specimens ARE stamped, the line-91 "not stamped" comment was stale) + a **capture-phase specimen-select listener** in `CommentHost` (Cmd/Ctrl-click). Self-gated on `isBareSpecimen()` at event time: a UI canvas has its own CanvasShell select router (specimens have none), so this is the ONLY select handler on a specimen and a pure no-op on a UI canvas — deliberately **NOT** added to the shared `COMMENT_CLAIMS` (which would `preventDefault`+`stopImmediatePropagation` a UI canvas's own select in capture phase — verified in input-router). `selSet.replace/add` auto-posts `dgn:'select-set'` (use-selection-set.tsx:132) → Inspector opens (Stage C) + edits persist.
- **E2** — new `MountSelectionHalo` (mirrors `MountHoverHalo`, steadier accent outline) + mounts `<ElementResizeOverlay />` **gated on an `isSpecimen` state** (specimen-only — a UI canvas already mounts one inside CanvasShell; double-mount would double the handles). Key enabler: `ElementResizeOverlay` is **camera-agnostic** (reads `useSelectionSet`/`useToolMode` + `getBoundingClientRect`, no viewport-controller dep) and lives in its own `use-element-resize.tsx`, so importing it into the lite mount adds ~8 KB and pulls in NO canvas-shell. `pickSpecimenEl` exclusion extended so the overlay chrome isn't itself selectable.
- **E3** — write path already generic over any cd-id; mechanism verified (select-set post + edit-css). Live specimen Cmd-click→select→edit deferred to the Stage-G scenario `specimen-and-media-editing`.
- **Gate:** tsc 6 baseline/0 new · biome clean · comment-mount.js rebuilt 42.5→50.6 KB (no shell bloat) · tests: comment-mount 5/5, canvas-hmr + comments-overlay + element-resize + camera-reveal 28/28. Files: `apps/studio/canvas-comment-mount.tsx` + `dist/comment-mount.js`.

Then G1 (DDR), G2 (tests), G4 (what's-new). Executing one stage at a time, checkpointing here.

🟢 **NEW-WRITE-ROUTE BATCH (backend layer) — DONE this session (2026-07-07, uncommitted on `main`).** Built the whole engine + route + undo + security-gate + test layer for the four structural/artboard ops; delete is fully UX-wired, the other three are backend-ready with shell plumbing but need canvas-side affordance UI (deferred within this batch).
> - **Key architecture decision:** the undo for ALL four new ops **reuses the existing reorder command** (`commands/reorder-command.ts`, `op:'reorder'` `record-edit` → whole-file `/_api/reorder-revert` seq swap) — NO new `commands/delete-element-command.ts`/`insert-element-command.ts` files were needed. The reorder command is already the generic "whole-file seq revert" command (the DDR-150 clip ops reuse it too via `logUndo`). A structural edit renumbers positional ids, so an inverse descriptor goes stale — exactly the reason reorder uses the server seq log — so reuse is correct, not a shortcut. This SUPERSEDES the plan's Task-I2 five-part-wiring assumption + the "Custom Components: delete/insert undo commands" row.
> - **Engine (`canvas-edit.ts`, pure + reparse-gated + on-disk async wrappers):** `applyDeleteElement`/`deleteElement` (spanWithFraming remove + resolveUsageId for shared-instance), `applyInsertElement`/`insertElement` (synthesize div/text/image w/o cd-id, place via applyMove's anchor+indent logic, recompute new post-transpile id by insert-offset match, image src contained), `applyResizeArtboard`/`resizeArtboard` (D4 — new `writeNumericAttr` writes `width={N}` NOT `"N"`; addresses `<DCArtboard>` by its `id` PROP since the rendered `<article data-dc-screen>` has no cd-id), `applyInsertArtboard`/`insertArtboard` (empty `<DCArtboard id label width height></DCArtboard>` after the last, dup-id + injection-guard). Helpers: `collectJsxByTag`, `synthInsertElement`, `InsertKind` type, `ARTBOARD_ID_RE`.
> - **api.ts:** `deleteElementOp`/`insertElementOp`/`insertArtboardOp`/`resizeArtboardOp` — reuse `resolveCanvasAbs` + `logUndo` + `history.writeSnapshot` + activity-rim suppress/unsuppress (exact reorder pattern); registered in the `Api` interface + returned object.
> - **http.ts:** `/_api/delete-element`, `/_api/insert-element`, `/_api/insert-artboard`, `/_api/resize-artboard` — MAIN-ORIGIN ONLY (absent from CANVAS_SAFE_API + startCanvasServer routes), `sameOriginWrite` CSRF + `isLoopbackHost` DNS-rebinding gated, 8 KB body cap. Added all four to `canvas-origin-gate.test.ts`'s main-origin allowlist (GET→403 from canvas origin).
> - **Client (`app.jsx`):** `structuralWrite` helper + `deleteElementShell`/`insertElementShell`/`insertArtboardShell`/`resizeArtboardShell` (POST + record-edit undo + best-effort re-select via pendingReorderRef) + refs; onMessage handlers `delete-request`/`insert-request`/`insert-artboard-request`/`resize-artboard-request`, each `e.source===activeWin` confused-deputy-gated + pinned to `activePath`.
> - **Canvas affordances (`canvas-shell.tsx` + `use-keyboard-discipline.tsx`):** element context-menu "Delete" (destructive) + "Insert ▸ Div/Text"; Del/Backspace key → `delete-request` (text-focus-guarded, `one.index` as idIndex = usage index for shared instances).
> - **Tests:** `test/element-structural-edit.test.ts` (15 pure-engine cases) + `test/element-structural-api.test.ts` (3 route round-trips incl. CSRF-403 + undo-revert + dup-422). Full: `bun tsc` 6/6 baseline 0 new · biome clean on touched TS · **element-structural + reorder-api + canvas-edit + canvas-origin-gate = 61/61**. Client bundle rebuilt `--release` (client.bundle.js 811 KB) + darwin-arm64 binary; **boot smoke:** health 200, all 4 handlers in served bundle, `/_api/delete-element` bad-id→400. **Live ⌘-click→delete/insert in WKWebView deferred to the Stage-G scenario** (cross-origin canvas selection — same ceiling as phase-12.1 drag). **UI-smoke gate note:** diff touches `apps/studio/**` but NOT rendering paths (canvas-lib/templates) — boot smoke covers "still renders"; full `/design:smoke` folded into the Stage-G scenario like Stages A–E.
> - **REMAINING in this batch:** I3 image-insert (needs Stage-F AssetPicker src) + tool-palette "+ Element" place-mode; I4 "+ Artboard" affordance + `SCREEN_PRESETS` picker + `patchCanvasMeta` grid; D4 `ElementResizeOverlay` artboard-scope extension (address by id prop, post `resize-artboard-request`). Then Stage F, then G3 security fan-out over the 4 new routes.

🟢 **STAGE G3 SECURITY — defender + attacker fan-out ran over the 5 new write routes; 2 Medium blockers found + CLOSED (2026-07-07, committed `b224328`).**
> - **Defender (`security-auditor`):** 0 blockers, 2 below-floor Lows. Confirmed the dual-allowlist (CANVAS_SAFE_API + startCanvasServer) holds, CSRF+loopback on every writer, `escapeAttr`+reparse gate unbypassable, `resolveCanvasAbs` doubly path-contained, client confused-deputy pinned to `activePath` (no `m.canvas` trust).
> - **Attacker (`ethical-hacker`):** **2 Medium BLOCKERS** (severityFloor=medium ⇒ gating) + 3 Lows + 1 chain:
>   - **BLOCKER 1 — excessive agency:** a hostile ACTIVE canvas can script-loop `delete-request`/`insert-*` with no user gesture (handlers gate only on `e.source===activeWin`+shape). **FIX:** server-side per-`createApi` **token bucket** (`takeStructuralToken`, burst 40 / refill 8/s, env `MAUDE_STRUCTURAL_BURST`) on all 4 structural ops → 429 when spent. Kills the silent-shred + fast-loop.
>   - **BLOCKER 2 — resource exhaustion:** structural writes had no budget — each snapshots a whole file to `_history/` (uncapped), whole-file RAM undo, no source ceiling. **FIX:** (a) **source-size ceiling** `MAX_CANVAS_SOURCE` (512 KB, env-tunable) refuses a GROWTH op (insert-element/insert-artboard) once source exceeds it → 413 (delete/shrink always pass); (b) **`_history` pruning** in `history.ts` (`maxSnapshotsPerSlug()`, 300 pairs, env `MAUDE_MAX_SNAPSHOTS`) bounds disk regardless of rate.
>   - **Lows hardened:** (4) `assertContainedAssetSrc` denylist → strict **allowlist** `^assets/[A-Za-z0-9._-]+$` (canvas-edit.ts); (5) `listAssets` `stat`→`lstat` (skip symlinks, api.ts); (3) AssetPicker now stamps `canvas: activePath` into the request + `onAssetPicked` **aborts if the active canvas drifted** while the modal was open (app.jsx). **Residual Low (documented, not fixed — below floor + needs a pre-existing local-write attacker outside DDR-054):** the `/assets/<file>` SERVE route still follows symlinks (no realpath-contain); the `escapeAttr` `&`-non-escape content-spoof nit. Both are follow-ups, not blockers.
> - **Tests added:** `test/element-structural-api.test.ts` +2 (rate-cap 429 via burst-2 boot; source-ceiling 413 + delete-still-allowed via ceiling-100 boot); `test/history-rollback.test.ts` +1 (prune to newest N, spaced writes). **Gate:** tsc 6/6 baseline 0 new · biome clean on touched TS (the 1 canvas-edit.ts:2451 warning is pre-existing, verified on HEAD) · **full dev-server suite 2081 pass / 1 skip / 1 fail** (the fail = pre-existing flaky `git-api.test.ts`, 32/0 clean in isolation). Client bundle release-rebuilt (`client.bundle.js` 815 KB; app.jsx picker-pin change).
> - **DDR note:** these caps + the specimen/knob/resize/structural model all fold into **Task G1 DDR-152** (still to write). The two Medium blockers are now CLOSED so the plan's G3 acceptance ("0 blockers at severityFloor") holds for the committed routes; re-run `/flow:validate-security` after F3/I4/D4 add the remaining affordances.
> - **Files touched (committed `b224328`):** `apps/studio/api.ts`, `canvas-edit.ts`, `history.ts`, `client/app.jsx`, `test/element-structural-api.test.ts`, `test/history-rollback.test.ts`, `dist/client.bundle.js`.

🟢 **STAGE H — reusable-component edit-scope predictability (INV-3) — H1+H2 DONE this session (2026-07-07, uncommitted on `main`); H3 next.**
> - **H1 — resolver + route (backend, fully tested).** New pure `resolveEditScope(canvasAbsPath, source, domId, renderedCount)` in `canvas-edit.ts` (exported, `+ interface EditScope`): composes the SAME primitives `resolveUsageId` ships — the element's enclosing `componentName` + the source usage count of that component. `local` when the element is top-level / single-usage & rendered 1×; `shared` ('component') when inside a component instantiated N× (`affects = usages`); `shared` ('mapped') when a single source element is rendered N× via `.map()` (DDR-139 §1 caveat — driven by the client-supplied `rendered`). Parse failure degrades to `local` (a badge must never crash selection). `editScopeOp` in `api.ts` (READ-only — no write/undo/rate-cap) + `Api` interface entry. **`GET /_api/edit-scope?canvas&id&rendered`** in `http.ts` — MAIN-ORIGIN ONLY (loopback-gated, no CSRF on a GET; added to `canvas-origin-gate.test.ts` allowlist → 403 from canvas origin). **Test:** new `test/edit-scope-api.test.ts` (boots a real server, serves a `Card`-reused-3× canvas, reads real cd-ids from `_locator.json`): inner-of-component → `shared`/`component`/affects 3; top-level `<section>` → `local`/`single`; `rendered=4` on a local id → `shared`/`mapped`/4; bogus id → 400. **1/1.**
> - **H2 — Inspector Local/Shared badge (client).** App fetches `/_api/edit-scope` on each single selection (debounced by selection id, `AbortController`-cancelled on change, `rendered=1` for v1 — the `.map()` refinement is a follow-up) → `editScope` state → `InspectorPanel`. New scope strip between the tab bar and body (visible across Inspect/Layers/CSS tabs, so it's never a surprise while editing on the Stage-C auto-opened CSS tab): "**Local · this element only**" (fg dot) vs "**Shared · `<Comp>` · edits N places**" (accent dot), with a `title` explaining that moving/resizing a whole instance stays local. New `.st-scope*` CSS in `3-shell-maude.css` (confirmed `--accent`/`--fg-3`/`--border-subtle` tokens, light+dark).
> - **Gate:** tsc 6/6 baseline **0 new** · biome clean on new code (api.ts import-order auto-organized; the lone `canvas-edit.ts:2514` `useOptionalChain` warning is the SAME pre-existing one, was 2451 on HEAD, shifted by the `resolveEditScope` insertion) · targeted suite **61/61** (edit-scope-api + canvas-origin-gate + canvas-edit + element-structural ×2) · client bundle + styles release-rebuilt (816 KB / 212 KB) · **boot smoke** (compiled binary :4487): health 200, `/_api/edit-scope?id=zzz`→400, served bundle carries `st-scope`/`edit-scope`, clean boot log. **Live badge-on-selection deferred to the Stage-G `structural-and-scope` scenario** (cross-origin selection ceiling — same as phase-12.1/Stages A–E).
> - **H3 (DONE 2026-07-07, committed `878c290`).** Route instance move/resize/reposition per-occurrence so a `<Card/>` INSTANCE stays local. Threaded an **optional GLOBAL DOM-occurrence `idIndex`** end-to-end: canvas posts it on `reposition-request` (`canvas-shell.tsx` onUp — hoisted the reorder `occ()` helper so both the outOfFlow reposition + reorder posts share it) + `resize-request` (`use-element-resize.tsx` — new `ElResizeDrag.idIndex = globalCdOccurrence(document, cdId, el)`, in both `postResizeRequest` + `postRotateRequest`) → shell `repositionElement`/`resizeElement` (app.jsx) put `idIndex` in the `/_api/edit-css` body → route (http.ts) → `editCss` (api.ts, validates int≥0) → `editAttribute`/`removeAttribute` → `applyEdit`/`applyRemove` **resolve `id = resolveUsageId(program, id, occ)` only when an occurrence is present**. So a whole-instance drag writes the left/top/width/height onto that instance's `<Component/>` usage (LOCAL); knob/paste-style pass NO occurrence → the write stays on the shared inner element (global-and-labeled by the H2 badge — the chosen model). No-op remap for a normal / single-usage / `.map()` element (resolveUsageId returns id). New `globalCdOccurrence()` in dom-selection.ts (co-located with `selectorIndex`; distinct from a Selection's artboard-SCOPED `index`) — matches the reorder drag's snapshot occurrence, in source order. **Test:** `test/edit-css-occurrence.test.ts` 4/4 (occurrence→usage; no-occurrence→inner; normal-element no-op; single-usage no-op). tsc 6/6 baseline **0 new**; biome **0 new** (the canvas-edit.ts format-drift at 830/2908 + the `useOptionalChain` warning shifted 2514→2536 BOTH pre-exist on HEAD — proven by `git stash`-ing my diff and re-running: HEAD already = 1 error + 1 warning). **Verification ceiling:** the live "drag one instance, others don't move" is the cross-origin drag ceiling (agent env can't script it) → Stage-G `structural-and-scope` scenario / user dogfood; the pure occurrence→usage routing is unit-proven. **Style-forwarding caveat (documented in-code):** routing writes `style` onto the `<Card/>` usage — visible movement needs the component to forward `style` to its root (real-lib parity); the correctness we deliver is LOCAL-not-global, per the plan's Option-A scope.

🟢 **STAGE M — flex / auto-layout editor — M1+M2+M3 DONE this session (2026-07-07, committed `90e0bd1`).** Client-only (no new routes → no security fan-out). Highest-value strategic gap per the deep-research pass.
> - **M1 — per-element Fixed / Hug / Fill sizing mode.** New PURE `sizing-mode.ts` (`sizingModeOf` classify + `sizingModePatch` deterministic writes + `isMainAxis`) — Figma parity: **Fixed** = explicit px (seeded from the rendered box), **Hug** = `fit-content`, **Fill** = context-aware (flex MAIN axis → `flex-grow:1`+`flex-basis:0%`+`<axis>:auto`; flex CROSS axis → `align-self:stretch`; block/grid child → `<axis>:100%`). The Fill context needs the PARENT's layout, which the shell can't read cross-origin later → new **`Selection.parentDisplay`/`parentFlexDirection`** captured canvas-side in `styleMapsFor` (one getComputedStyle on `parentElement`) and threaded through. CssKnobs `applySizing(axis, mode)` applies the patch over the existing commit/reset lanes (per-prop edit-css + undo record; server per-file lock serializes → coherent box). Segmented W/H control at the top of the Size section + new `min-width`/`min-height`/`max-height` rows + a flex-CHILD row group (`align-self`/`flex-grow`/`flex-shrink`/`flex-basis`) gated on `parentDisplay` being flex. KNOB_PROPS extended with all of these + `flex`/`flex-wrap`. **Test:** `test/sizing-mode.test.ts` 18/18 (main/cross axis, classify, all patch shapes incl. fill-prop clearing + px clamp).
> - **M2 — grouped auto-layout editor.** Layout section now shows the flex vocabulary (Direction · Wrap · Distribution · Align · Gap) **only when the element IS a flex/grid container** (flex-direction/flex-wrap flex-only; align-items/justify-content/gap also for grid); a non-container gets a one-click **"+ Auto layout (flex)"** CTA (sets `display:flex`). `justify-content` already carried space-between/around/evenly; added `flex-wrap` (new `CSS_FLEX_WRAP`) + `align-self` (`CSS_ALIGN_SELF`). ## /flow:done — feature-whiteboard-ai-toolkit (2026-07-07)

✅ **Validate: 5-agent fan-out.** scenario-runner authored + ran a real new scenario (`whiteboard-ai-loop`, web-desktop, 0 blockers, parity OK — `.ai/scenarios/whiteboard-ai-loop/`); a11y-auditor 0 blockers (N/A, no rendering surface); design-system-guard 0 violations. security-auditor + ethical-hacker together found **4 concrete bugs, all fixed + regression-tested**, plus **1 architectural residual** the user explicitly chose to ship with documented:
1. Path traversal in `_canvas-rects-static.mjs` (`--rel` had no containment check) — fixed with a `resolve()`+`startsWith(designRootAbs+sep)` assert mirroring `inspect.ts`'s `mtimeFor()`.
2. `GlobalRegistrator.register()`'s fetch monkey-patch persisting past its use in `annotate.mjs`'s `ensureFullStrokes()` — fixed via `register()`→parse→`unregister()` immediately, before the batch's later loopback-gated PUT. (A first attempt at a narrower fix — patching only `globalThis.DOMParser` on a scoped `happy-dom` `Window` instead of full `GlobalRegistrator` — silently broke parsing, since happy-dom's internals reach through `this.window` for more than just `DOMParser`; caught via the existing test suite, reverted to the register/unregister pairing.)
3. `--board`/`--flow`'s `groups`/`cards` caps (from the original Task 6 build) didn't cover `connections[]`/`edges[]` — fixed with `BOARD_MAX_CONNECTIONS`(400)/`FLOW_MAX_EDGES`(400), the edge cap enforced once inside the shared `flowToOps()` (also closed a **pre-existing** gap: the top-level `--flow` verb never had a node/edge cap at all before this pass).
4. `MAX_ANNOTATIONS_BYTES` was only checked on merged OUTPUT, never on read — fixed with a read-time size check in `main()` before the file reaches `ctx.rawSvg`.
5. **Residual, accepted**: `canvas-rects`/`--rects` surfacing up to 400 elements' real DOM text (synced to peers by default, DDR-079) widens DDR-085's already-open trifecta residual. Not architecturally closeable from this feature (needs a session/orchestration-layer control). Mitigated with an explicit MUST-NOT rule in `skill whiteboard`'s trust model; **user asked directly, chose "ship with it documented"** (matches DDR-085's own precedent).
6. **Packaging gap found + fixed**: `apps/studio/package.json` is a nested workspace manifest the npm tarball doesn't ship (confirmed via `npm pack --dry-run` AND against a real global install of `@1agh/maude@0.40.0` on this machine — no `node_modules` in its `apps/studio/`). `ensureFullStrokes()`'s `@happy-dom/global-registrator` import is likely the *first* runtime third-party-package need in this bin-script family not routed through the `BOOT_VERBS` compiled-binary path — would have thrown an unhandled crash for a real end user's first `move`/`set-text`/`set-color`. Fixed with a try/catch around the dynamic import giving a clear, actionable error (use `delete`+`create` instead) rather than a raw stack trace — this is a **graceful-degrade fix, not a resolution of the underlying packaging gap**; a proper fix (bundling it into the compiled binary path, or another distribution mechanism) is unassessed follow-up work, flagged in DDR-151.
7. Also caught + fixed: `happy-dom`/`@happy-dom/global-registrator` pinned exact (`20.10.1`, was `^20.9.0`) given happy-dom's 3 disclosed 2025-2026 CVEs in the "parses untrusted content" category (all patched at pinned version); `@happy-dom/global-registrator` correctly lives in `devDependencies` again (test-only usage — `happy-dom` itself is the one needed at runtime, moved to `dependencies`).

All fixes verified via a SECOND independent re-verification round (security-auditor + ethical-hacker again) — both original fixes confirmed holding; the second round is what surfaced items 3, 4, 6, 7 above. Lint: clean (0 errors; a 2-file biome format nit from the fix-round was auto-fixed via `pnpm format`). `code-simplifier` agent type unavailable in this session's registry — skipped, relying on lint + the two security passes' own code-clarity notes instead.

### Video-comp edit-persistence smoke + re-verify (2026-07-04, branch `main`)
User still reported "letter color reverts on replay/loop" AFTER the round-1 fix. **Exhaustively re-verified on the current build — edits persist in EVERY path** (agent-browser, real edit-css): source write persists; fresh load renders it; standalone shell live-reload PAUSED + PLAYING; **full-app canvas-origin iframe** (localhost:split) live-reload; and the hardest case — **edit color WHILE the comp is looping in the full app → letters stayed cyan through pause+seek**. Conclusion: the permanent-revert cause was the `editStyleProp` double-comma (a build failure → Player rendered OLD source), **fixed in `83931ce`** — a stale bundle would still show it (⌘R / restart to get the current build). Added `test/edit-persistence.test.ts` (3, CI-safe): a CSS edit (swap / insert-next-to-trailing-comma / edit sequence) must survive into the **built module** (source→edit→buildCanvasModule→assert value present) — the guard that would have caught the original bug (`opacity: o, , color` → build throw → old render). 38/0 with canvas-edit. No source/bundle change this round; demo letter color reset to `#f0ece5`.

### Video-comp: 4 dogfood bugs fixed (agent-browser verified) (2026-07-04, branch `main`)
User hit 4 bugs; all reproduced + fixed live via agent-browser (canvas-origin split). (1) **Timeline didn't follow the artboard** — root: canvas-lib computes `activeArtboardId` (nearest viewport centre, drives the blue ring) but never reported it to the shell; added a `dgn:'active-artboard'` postMessage on change → app tracks `canvasActiveArtboard` (active-window gated) and the timeline re-parses to it (over `selected.artboardId`). **Verified: pan intro-centre → 112f Intro/Payoff; pan reel-centre → 228f TitleCard+music.** (2) **ClipShot black + (3) no audio** — SAME root: the hardened canvas-origin CSP had `default-src 'none'` and **no `media-src`**, so every `<Video>`/`<Audio>` was blocked. Added `media-src 'self' blob:` (http.ts) — same inert-bytes trust as `img-src 'self'`. **Verified: reel renders the real footage; music element plays (currentTime 3.48→4.88s) once the iframe has sticky activation from a canvas click.** (3b) added a **volume slider** to the transport (posts `timeline-volume`→`PlayerRef.setVolume`; unmutes on drag). (4) **Resizable timeline** — drag-the-top-edge handle (`onResize`, clamp 140–640px). **Verified: 216→376px, all rows + music waveform visible.** `canvas-origin-gate.test.ts` +media-src assertion. Full suite **1899 pass/1 skip/0 fail**; client rebuilt --release. Note: preview audio needs the sticky in-iframe gesture (a canvas click) before programmatic play emits sound; exported-mp4 audio-mux still open.

### Video-comp: timeline-as-control-surface (2026-07-04, branch `main`)
Dogfood follow-ups on the 2-comp canvas: (1) **Timeline follows the selected artboard** — `parseCompTimeline(src, total, selectedArtboardId)` tags each `<VideoComp>` with its enclosing `<DCArtboard id>` and scopes to the SELECTED artboard's comp (falls back to media/duration/first); app fetches source once then re-parses on `selected` change, so switching artboards redraws the timeline. (2) **Player chrome removed from the artboard** — `VideoComp` `controls` now defaults OFF (`(controls ?? false) && !capturing`); the Timeline is the sole transport. (3) **Sound moved to the Timeline** — a mute button in the transport (shown when the comp has audio) posts `timeline-mute`/`timeline-volume` to the scoped comp (`PlayerRef.mute/unmute/setVolume`); play syncs mute first. Also (prior, same session) fixed the timeline driving ALL comps (id-targeted messages, commit 21c5257) + switched the reel to `<Video>` (reliable Player render). **Known:** preview audio is subject to browser autoplay policy — a cross-origin canvas iframe needs an in-iframe user gesture (the user's canvas click) before programmatic play emits sound; and the EXPORTED mp4 is still silent (audio-mux is the open TODO). `timeline-parse.test.ts` +1 (artboard scope). Full suite **1899 pass/1 skip/0 fail**; client rebuilt --release. Demo canvas (Video switch + no chrome) stays local.

### Video-comp real-footage reel + music layer (2026-07-04, branch `main`)
User: a 2nd artboard on the intro canvas = a REAL MP4 strih (downloaded clips) + light motion graphics, + a music bed visible as a timeline layer. **Shipped + live-verified.** Code (the feature): (1) **canvas-origin video/audio serving** — `.mp4/.m4v/.mov/.webm/.mp3/.wav/.m4a/.ogg` added to `http.ts` MIME + `CANVAS_ASSET_EXTS` so a comp `<Video>`/`<Audio>` loads from `assets/` inside the untrusted iframe (inert bytes, nosniff); (2) **timeline music layer** — `timeline-parse.js` now emits `audio[]` rows from `<Audio src>` + `TimelinePanel` renders a distinct teal waveform row (`♪ music.mp3`); (3) **multi-comp scoping** — the parser picks the media comp (Video/Audio) among several `<VideoComp>`s + reads its total/fps, and resolves EXPRESSION consts (`const TOTAL = A+B-XF`) so a derived duration parses; (4) **video-seek-aware capture** — `bin/_video-playwright.mjs` comp-mode now waits for every `<video>` to land on its seeked frame (readyState/`seeked`, 1.5 s cap) before the screenshot — else the export catches stale frames. `timeline-parse.test.ts` +2 (audio row, multi-comp scope). **Live:** reel renders 3 real clips (drone→jellyfish→animation) with fade/slide/wipe + lower-thirds + progress bar + REC HUD + end card; timeline shows 5 seq rows + the `♪ music.mp3` row @ 228f; exported MP4 (1.5 MB, 7.6 s) verified to contain the real footage + overlays baked in (sampled clip A + the B→C wipe). Full suite **1896 pass/1 skip/0 fail**; client rebuilt --release. **Demo held OUT of git** (canvas + ~6.5 MB downloaded stock footage + export) pending the user's call — public repo + third-party clip licensing.

### Video-comp dogfood feedback round 2 (2026-07-04, branch `main`)
Two follow-ups — both live-verified:
1. ✅ **Drag-to-retime.** Server: `applyRetimeSequence`/`retimeSequence` (canvas-edit.ts) rewrites the index-th sequence's `durationInFrames`/`from`, **const-preferring** (`durationInFrames={A}` → bumps `const A = …`, so a derived `const TOTAL = A+B-XF` moves in lock-step) + literal fallback + reparse gate; `api.retimeSequenceOp` (resolveCanvasAbs + clamp + activity-suppress + pre-retime snapshot); `/_api/retime-sequence` route (main-origin, `sameOriginWrite` CSRF, origin-gate 403 asserted). Client: TimelinePanel resize handle on each block → live optimistic width → release → POST → file-watcher reload → re-announce → re-parse. **Live: dragged Intro handle → `const A` 66→77 → comp total 112→123f (derived TOTAL auto-updated) → block widened.** `canvas-edit.test.ts` +3 (const-bump/literal+from/out-of-range). Also fixed the bottom-scrub label-gutter offset.
2. ✅ **Timeline no longer closes when a right panel opens.** It's a bottom dock — removed it from the right-rail mutual-exclusion (`openRightPanel`/`toggleRightPanel`), added standalone `toggleTimeline`. **Live: Inspector + Timeline open simultaneously.**
**Gate:** full suite **1893 pass / 1 skip / 0 fail**; client bundle rebuilt --release; floors 22/22; biome clean. **Next slice:** keyframe-level drag (move an interpolate window), `from`-drag for plain Sequences. Committing now; intro demo A reverted to 66 (test artifact), stays uncommitted.

### Video-comp dogfood feedback round 1 (2026-07-04, branch `main`)
User dogfooded the video feature + gave 4 points — all addressed + live-verified:
1. ✅ **Timeline → bottom dock.** Moved from the right dock to a full-width horizontal strip below the stage (app.jsx layout + TimelinePanel rewrite + 3-shell-maude.css). Live: `bottom=870/900, fullWidth`.
2. ✅ **Clicking artboard no longer plays.** `video-comp.tsx` Player: `clickToPlay={false}` + `spaceKeyToPlayOrPause={false}` + default `autoPlay=false` (opens PAUSED on a ~35% poster frame → editable). Play only via the transport button. Live: clicking canvas center left the frame at 50 (not advancing).
3. ✅ **Sequence + keyframe rows visible.** New `/_api/canvas-source` read-only route (main-origin, `loadCanvasSource`+containment, origin-gate 403 asserted) → `client/panels/timeline-parse.js` parses `<TransitionSeries.Sequence>`/`<Sequence>` (const-resolving, TransitionSeries overlap math) + per-component `interpolate(frame,[a,b])` windows → one row per sequence with keyframe bars + click-to-seek. Live: 2 rows (Intro/Payoff), 5 keyframes, click Payoff → seek frame 50 (playhead aligns). `test/timeline-parse.test.ts` (5).
4. ✅ **CSS edit now persists across replay.** Root cause = `editStyleProp` (canvas-edit.ts) unconditionally prepended `, ` when appending a style prop → `opacity: o, , color:…` (double comma) on a frame-driven style with a trailing comma → build failed → Player rendered OLD source → "reset". Fixed with a trailing-comma guard + regression test. (Also fixed the user's broken intro file.)
**Gate:** full suite **1890 pass / 1 skip / 0 fail**; client bundle rebuilt --release; runtime floors 22/22; biome error-level clean. **v1 note:** rows are read-only (see+seek); drag-to-retime a block / move a keyframe (source-patch) is the next slice. Committing now; intro demo canvas + `.design/exports/*.mp4|gif` stay uncommitted (user's call).
