**Done 2026-07-01 (branch `main`):** **ACP composer slash-command autocomplete + inline highlight — CLOSED via /flow:done.** Follow-up to the phase-31 (DDR-123) native Assistant chat panel + the 2026-07-01 empty-state CTA (`417079f`). Typing `/` in the composer opens a filter-as-you-type popover of the design + flow commands the user's own `claude` actually has (↑↓/Enter/Tab/Esc + click; prefill-only, never auto-send), and a command that exists lights up as a pill INSIDE the input via a mirror-overlay. **Source of truth for "exists":** the ACP `available_commands_update` (previously received-and-dropped) — captured in `acp/bridge.ts` (`onCommands` + `warmUp(chatId)` = session-create, no prompt), cached process-wide + broadcast as a `{t:'commands'}` frame + replayed on open (`acp/index.ts`, new `{t:'warm'}` inbound frame), surfaced client-side (`acp-runtime.js`) and merged with a curated static bootstrap list (`client/panels/slash-commands.js`, pure/unit-tested). **T8 live-verified against real `claude`:** the catalogue fires on `session/new` alone (98 cmds), names are colon+prefix (`design:edit`) so `normalizeName` needed no massaging. **Gate:** full studio suite **1727 pass / 1 skip / 0 fail** · biome clean · 13 new tests · client bundle rebuilt `--release` (741,983 B; runtime bundles preserved at HEAD after a full-suite dev-server-boot self-heal clobbered `dist/` to dev-form — the CLAUDE.md trap, recovered via `git checkout HEAD -- dist/` + client-only `--release`). **Security fan-out (defender PASS 0-blockers + adversarial 3-blockers):** the attacker found a VERIFIED **pre-existing CSWSH gap** — `/_ws/acp` upgrade gated on loopback-Host only, no Origin check, so a cross-origin drive-by could open the privileged socket (WS bypasses SOP) and drive my new `warm`/`commands` frames (silent spawn + private-catalogue disclosure). **FIXED before close-out:** `isSameOriginWs` Origin gate on the `/_ws/acp` upgrade (`ws.ts`/`server.ts`) — verified-safe by construction (native app navigates to `http://localhost:<port>` → same-origin; drive-by rejected) + unit + booted-server cross-origin-403 tests; this also hardens the pre-existing cross-origin `prompt` reachability (phase-31 F2). Residual: `warm`/`prompt` bridge race (Finding C) now same-origin-only → tracked; spawn-cap (F3)/transcript-cap (F4)/record-warm-in-transcript = tracked follow-ups. Changeset (minor) + What's New (`slash-command-autocomplete`, pending) authored. **Ceiling:** interactive popover/pill render = native `.app` dogfood (`isNativeApp()`-gated; not agent-browser-drivable) — testids added (`chat-cmd-menu`/`chat-cmd-item-*`/`chat-cmd-pill`) for a follow-up desktop-e2e scenario. **Shared-tree note:** a concurrent session was live-editing `canvas-shell.tsx` (phase-12.1 rework) — excluded from this commit; my bundle stayed clean of it (`client.bundle.js` entry is only `client/app.jsx`; canvas-shell compiles into the separate iframe runtime). _Plan archived: `.ai/plans/archive/feature-acp-slash-command-autocomplete.md`._ Commit-in-place on `main`; push/PR = owner's call (main PR-protected).

**Done 2026-06-30 (branch `main`):** **Git version-switcher: fast, trustworthy, git-native (DDR-133) — CLOSED via /flow:done.** Follow-up to the two 2026-06-29 plans (DDR-131 remote-drafts, DDR-132 identity/probe cache) on 6 user-reported switcher problems. **Headline (DDR-107 end-state):** runtime-detect system `git` and prefer it for the READ paths (status/listBranches/log/diff/show/unpushed/currentBranch) AND the network paths (fetch/ahead-behind) — iso-git's `statusMatrix`/`listBranches` was *crashing* (`No obj for …`) + slow on a real 306-branch repo (the true root cause of the vanishing dropdown, found via dev:desktop logs); measured status 87 ms / branches 62 ms via system git vs crash+10 s-timeout on iso. iso still backs the WRITE paths (commit/checkout/branch/fold/push/pull) unless `MAUDE_USE_SYSTEM_GIT=1`. + 12 s/8 s timeouts on the two network paths; `organization_member` + pagination in `listUserRepos` (org repos now discoverable); **git-native switcher vocabulary** (branches by name, `main` = "default branch", "Merge this branch → main") superseding the DDR-110/119 plain layer for this surface (user's /flow:plan fork); phantom `origin` row fixed. **Gate:** git/github suites green per-file under BOTH engines (default auto-detect + `MAUDE_NO_SYSTEM_GIT=1`); full studio suite 1688/1689 (1 pre-existing flaky git-api round-trip timeout under load); biome clean; parity OK. **Security (defender PASS + adversarial):** DDR-131 RCE/local-read posture holds (classify gate before the engine branch on every sink); adversarial found **1 real DDR-133 regression — F1 (Medium): the unattended ahead/behind probe's `!trustedHttp` host-allowlist sat AFTER the engine branch, so with system git auto-preferred a poisoned non-github `remote.origin.url` got an unattended `git fetch` (SSRF/beacon) — FIXED before commit** (hoist the guard, mirror `gitFetchRemote`; + regression test); defender's listUserRepos `Array.isArray` robustness applied. Reports `.ai/logs/security-reviews/ddr-133-system-git-{defender,attacker}.md` (gitignored). **Verification — DOM-driven desktop-e2e (built on the just-committed harness `fbaf628`):** 3 native-app scenarios (`git-branch-switcher` · `git-lifecycle` = new-branch/switch-branches/commit/push/fetch/merge-to-main/pull against a real github clone with `MAUDE_USE_SYSTEM_GIT=1` → credential helper · `git-switch-repos` via `open_local_project`, retried), 12 evidence screenshots; testids on switcher + GitPanel; 3 harness gotchas added to the skill. DDR-133 + What's New entry + 3 scenario specs. _Plan archived: `.ai/plans/archive/feature-git-switcher-fast-and-trustworthy.md`._ Commit-in-place on `main`; push/PR = owner's call (main PR-protected).

**Done 2026-06-24 (branch `main`):** **First-open AI-editing readiness check + sidecar PATH fix — CLOSED via /flow:done (DDR-128).** A non-blocking, detect-and-guide readiness check surfaces the AI-editing dependency chain so `/design:edit` stops silently no-opping in the packaged app. **Root-cause fix:** a Finder/Dock-launched `.app` inherited the truncated launchd PATH, so the ACP adapter + the paired `claude` + its `maude design <verb>` shell-outs all failed to resolve their binaries (worked under `tauri dev`, broke when bundled) — `sidecar.rs` now resolves the user's login-shell PATH and injects it into the dev-server sidecar (one fix corrects the whole chain). **New surface:** `GET /_api/preflight` (`readiness.ts`) — read-only probe of `claude`/`maude` on PATH + a `~/.claude` plugin-registry scan (`design@maude`/`flow@maude`) + optional `agent-browser`, main-origin-only (off `CANVAS_SAFE_API`, DDR-088). **Three UI surfaces** share one `ReadinessList`: a non-blocking strip in the onboarding wizard, the ChatPanel not-connected explainer, and a new **Help → Check AI editing readiness…** modal (the persistent re-check). Docs (`desktop/index.mdx`) now state the AI-editing prerequisite. **/done gate:** full dev-server suite **1665 pass / 1 skip / 0 fail** · readiness + origin-gate green · biome exit 0 · parity OK. **Security fan-out (defender PASS-with-suggestions + attacker):** attacker found 1 MEDIUM — the probe shelled out **synchronously** (`Bun.spawnSync`) with no Origin gate, so on a fresh machine a single call froze the event loop ~5 s/binary and a drive-by page could spawn-storm the loopback port — **FIXED before close-out** (async `Bun.spawn` + the three probes run concurrently + `sameOriginWrite` rejects cross-origin GETs + a regression test); DDR-128's security posture corrected to note the login-shell rc now runs on GUI launch. Reports: `.ai/logs/security-reviews/ddr-128-readiness-{defender,attacker}.md`. Changeset (minor) + What's New (pending) authored. **Ceiling:** the native `.app` PATH-fix (Finder launch) + the readiness UI in WKWebView are user dogfood (agent-browser can't flip `isNativeApp()`); built the `.app` + a sandbox-launch recipe so the fresh-user empty state can be previewed without uninstalling anything. **Done 2026-06-23 (branch `native-app`):** **Phase-32 — Native Maude: distribution, auto-update & maintenance — CLOSED via /flow:done.** Maude is now an installable, self-updating desktop app for macOS + Windows with zero terminal. All 7 tasks: (1) **auto-update** — `tauri-plugin-updater` polls a signed Vercel feed (`site/app/releases/[target]/[arch]/[current_version]/route.ts` reads GitHub Releases → signed JSON), downloads in the background, shows a non-blocking "Maude updated · restart to apply" banner; the check/download/install loop is **Rust-only** (`updater.rs`) so the remote origin gets only a **guarded** `restart_to_update` (no-op unless an update is staged); ed25519 pubkey pinned in `tauri.conf.json` (keypair generated this phase — private half → owner's GitHub Secrets). (2) **Windows `.msi` CI** — `build-desktop.yml` is now a macOS+Windows matrix; both legs sign the updater artifacts; Authenticode signing conditional on `WINDOWS_CERTIFICATE` (absent → unsigned, like phase-26). (3) **`/desktop`** platform-detected download page + docs + nav link. (4) **opt-in crash reporting** — local-file backend (chosen over Sentry): `prefs.rs` + `crash_reporter.rs` panic hook, default OFF, scrubbed (no canvas/paths/tokens), capped at 10, first-run checkbox. (5) **What's New** re-checks on window focus. (6) `tauri.conf.json` + `Cargo.toml` join the version-parity set (`bump-version.sh` + `check-version-parity.sh`). (7) What's New entry + hub-docs note. **DDR-126** (distribution model + signing-key custody + local-crash-over-Sentry + security posture; backfilled the DDR index 122–126). **Gate:** parity ✅ · biome ✅ · dev-server **1656/1 skip/0** · CLI **163/163** (after a `better-sqlite3` from-source rebuild fixed an ABI mismatch — env, not code) · site build ✅. **Security (defender PASS + adversarial):** 2 HIGH — F1 (main-origin CSP gap, DDR-109 F2, **pre-existing**) had its phase-introduced restart-DoS **FIXED** (guard) + W2/W3/W4 fixed; CSP itself = priority follow-up; F7 (single feed endpoint) availability-only — a feed attacker can't push updates without the signing key — failover tracked. Reports local in `.ai/logs/security-reviews/phase-32-distribution{,-attacker}.md` (gitignored). **Drive-by:** `/design:chat` added to the site command catalog (phase-31 drift that reddened the build) + biome-format on 2 stray phase-31 test files. **Model (2 rules):** (R1) vidíš jen canvasy své branche (git default, nepřebíjet — žádné cross-branch items); (R2) multiplayer = stejná branch (kurzory+anotace+komentáře+TSX live jen na stejné branchi). Editace na stejném HEAD smí oba; commit/push sociálně koordinovaný; nový canvas přes „Načíst". Soft editing-presence = heads-up, NE zeď, NE lock.

**Dogfood round 1 (2026-06-18, user on `pnpm dev:desktop`):** sign-in WORKED end-to-end (device code → connected) once the ACL was fixed. **ACL fix (the crux):** Tauri v2 rejects custom-command invokes from a REMOTE origin (the dev-server `http://localhost:<port>` the webview loads) unless the resolved ACL grants the command — `remote.urls` alone is insufficient. Fix = declare the app commands in `build.rs` (`AppManifest::commands`) → generates `allow-*` permissions → grant them in `capabilities/default.json` (+ `remote.urls` loopback wildcard). Validated at build (unknown permission fails the build). **6 polish fixes, all DS-compliant (user: "use the maude DS on all elements"):** (1) modals + dropdown were TRANSPARENT — root cause: `className="panel"` referenced the CANVAS-side `_components.css` `.panel`, absent from the client bundle → no background. Reworked to the shell DS `.st-dialog`/`.st-scrim` treatment (solid `var(--bg-1)` + border + shadow, 72% scrim). (2) identity moved from a big top card to a **compact avatar docked at the sidebar BOTTOM**, menu opens upward. (3) create + share dialogs solid + polished. (4) "Open a project" (broken external-link list, redundant with native File ▸ Open Project) reframed to **"Pull a local copy"**: pick repo → native folder picker (`pick_directory` Tauri cmd) → clone to chosen disk path (`gitClone` iso-git + `POST /_api/github/clone`, path-validated) → switch project (`open_local_project` Tauri cmd). (5) browser studio renders NO identity bar (CLI-launched from an already-set-up repo). 2 new Tauri commands declared+granted in build.rs/capability; canvas-origin-gate asserts `/_api/github/clone` →403. github-api 24/1-skip, gate 31 expects, tsc+biome clean, cargo check ✓, bundle rebuilt. **Caveat:** running a source-tree dev-server against this repo re-churns `dist/runtime/*.js` to dev bundles — restored to committed release each time. _Pending dogfood: the full pull-local-copy clone+switch (folder picker → clone → open)._

Live-verified: both panes render the full live canvas chrome (toolbar/inspect/world). (2) **Changes panel absent in `pnpm dev:desktop`** — root cause: the Tauri sidecar is a compiled binary and `dev:desktop` had **`beforeDevCommand: null`**, so it ran the STALE pre-phase-27 binary (Jun 17). Fix: synced the fresh binary + staged resources (both gitignored), and set `beforeDevCommand = sync-sidecar && stage-resources` (mirrors build) so dev:desktop always uses the latest BUILT binary + client bundle. **Caveat for the user:** after dev-server *source* changes, the binary must be REBUILT (`cd apps/studio && bun run build.ts --release`) before relaunching — sync only copies the existing dist binary. The Changes panel is a View-menu toggle (**View ▸ Changes** / **⌘⇧G**), not shown by default.

**`/flow:validate` (2026-06-17):** static green — biome lint/format (format fixed 1 PRE-EXISTING file `apps/desktop/src-tauri/capabilities/default.json`, phase-26, unrelated) · CLI 163/163 · dev-server 1522/1522 (1 flaky sync-timing fail cleared on re-run) · site build OK · parity/tarball/tokens/site-content all ✓ · config schemaErrors=0 (driftCount=1 warn-only). **Fan-out found REAL blockers → all FIXED in-session + re-verified:**
- **Security FAIL→CLEARED:** defender+attacker both found **A1 (HIGH) argument injection** — unvalidated `remote`/`ref` → `git push/pull/fetch` argv with no `--` (system-git path; attacker confirmed `--upload-pack=` executes). Plus **A2 (MED)** diff-sha leading-dash, **A3 (MED)** token-in-GET-query, **A4 (low)** DiffView main-origin iframe for untrusted conflict path. **Fixed:** `safeGitArg`/`safeRemoteArg` (reject dash-led + `:` + path-shaped remote) at the endpoint boundary (closes both engines); anchored diff-sha regex; removed `?token=` from status GET (phase-28 keychain instead); DiffView iframe only renders `.tsx`/`.html`. + 4 regression tests. **Focused re-verify: A1/A2/A3 all CLOSED, medium floor CLEARED, 0 blockers.**
- **a11y 2 blockers FIXED:** M/A badge text failed 4.5:1 in LIGHT theme (`--bg-0` white-on-mid-tone) → fixed dark ink `oklch(0.16…)` clears all 4 in both themes (live-verified light+dark); DiffView modal had no Esc/focus-mgmt → added Esc-close + focus-in/restore (live-verified). + textarea aria-label.
- **DS 1 blocker FIXED:** hover-hidden `.gp-row-act` had no `:focus-visible` reveal → added (mirrors `.st-row-del` prior). + dv-sheet shadow → `var(--shadow-lg)`.
- **Scenario:** no formal `.ai/scenarios/` entry — substituted by full agent-browser live verification (badges live, save/clear, history, publish-authRequired, runtime-filter, both themes, DiffView Esc). Web-desktop-only feature (no cross-platform parity dimension). **Recommend `/scenario new git-changes-panel` for repeatable regression.**

Remaining for `/done`: author a changeset (changesets provider; user-visible feature) + What's New entry; optional formal scenario. **Post-re-verify hardening (the re-verify's top follow-up, now done):** added a SERVICE-LEVEL second guard `isSafeGitPositional` in `service.ts` (pushSystem/pullSystem/remoteAheadBehindSystem/diffSystem) so the endpoint regex is no longer the SOLE guard against A1 — a future endpoint-regex relaxation can't silently re-open arg-injection (1523/1523 tests green). Accepted non-blocking below-floor warnings (deliberately left, documented): DiffView radiogroup arrow-key nav (keyboard-operable, not roving-tabindex), dv-thumb placeholder gradient (deliberate coming-soon for the deferred historical render), sub-44px tap targets (dense desktop shell idiom), raw-stderr error fallback (git doesn't echo the token), token in the system-git child's argv/`ps` (known iso-git tradeoff, single-user loopback), History scroll-container not keyboard-focusable + short-sha title (low), `status?remote=1` unauthenticated iso-git fetch (informational, phase-28 note). **User dogfood 2026-06-16 (`pnpm tauri dev` on real machine) → 2 fixes + 1 open:**
- FIXED **crash**: `tauri-plugin-deep-link` aborted in `did_finish_launching` (Apple-Event open handler) on the non-bundled dev binary (SIGABRT) → removed the plugin; `maude://` deferred to phase-29 (needs bundled .app + open?path= route).
- FIXED **duplicate browser window**: dev-server auto-opened the system browser on boot → sidecar now spawns with `NO_OPEN=1` (server.ts honors it). Also added `MAUDE_CANVAS_ORIGIN_SPLIT` passthrough to the sidecar for WKWebView debugging.
- RESOLVED **"canvas won't open"**: it was downstream of the deep-link crash, not an iframe bug. **User confirmed 2026-06-16:** with the secure default (cross-origin split **ON**, DDR-063) the canvas renders in WKWebView via `pnpm dev:desktop`. The flagged highest-risk item (DDR-063 cross-origin iframe in WKWebView) WORKS with the DDR-109 CSP. No origin-split downgrade needed.
- ADDED root scripts `pnpm dev:desktop` / `pnpm build:desktop` (prepend `~/.cargo/bin` to PATH; `MAUDE_PROJECT_ROOT` overridable).

**Phase-26 committed on `native-app` (`632fbdd`).** Tasks 1–6 done (5 + canvas render user-confirmed in WKWebView); Task 7 (CI) authored + YAML-valid.

**Follow-up fix (uncommitted, 2026-06-16) — "Open Project always crashes":** root cause was `app.restart()` in the File ▸ Open Project handler — on the non-bundled `tauri dev` binary the relaunch + macOS "open" Apple Event aborts `tao` in `did_finish_launching` (SIGABRT; the same `_handleAEOpenEvent` signature the deep-link plugin hit, which is why removing deep-link didn't fix it). Fix: `sidecar::switch_project` switches the project IN-PROCESS (mutable `project_root: Mutex<String>` → kill child → supervisor respawns with new root → re-navigate webview), no relaunch. Compiles + launches clean; the GUI folder-picker switch is pending user live-test before committing. DDR-106 addendum updated.

**Follow-up fix #2 (uncommitted, 2026-06-17) — intermittent white screen on launch:** a stale `<project>/.design/_server.json` from a prior session was read by `wait_for_server` instantly, so the webview navigated to the OLD port before this run's server bound it → connection refused → white screen ("občas"). Fix: clear `_server.json` in `lib.rs` setup BEFORE spawning the sidecar (mirrors what `switch_project` already does), so the navigate always waits for THIS run's fresh post-bind write. **Verified by agent:** planted a stale `_server.json` (port 9999) → app correctly navigated to the fresh 4399, not 9999; clean shutdown, no panic. Both follow-up fixes (#1 in-process switch + #2 stale-file clear) committed + pushed (`0e35705`).

**Phase-26 COMPLETED 2026-06-17 — working `.dmg`.** `tauri build` → `Maude_0.30.0_aarch64.dmg` (29.5 MB), bundled-app runtime verified. The crux was that the `bun --compile` sidecar serves its assets from DISK (not embedded), and walk-up can't find them inside `Maude.app`. Fix (DDR-106 addendum #4): `paths.ts` `MAUDE_DEV_SERVER_ROOT` override + `stage-resources.mjs`/`tauri.conf` `bundle.resources` ship `apps/studio/{dist,client}` + `plugins/design/templates` into `Resources/` + `sidecar.rs` sets the env from `resource_dir()`. **Gotcha hit + recorded:** the committed `apps/studio/dist/maude-<slug>` binary was stale (pre-override) → had to rebuild it (`bun run build.ts --release`) before bundling. Bundled smoke: `/`, `/_client/client.bundle.js`, `/_canvas-runtime/react.js` all 200; sidecar boots first try. **Follow-up (user dogfood):** canvas browser loaded but opening a canvas → "Importing a module script failed" — the bundle shipped only `dist/`+`client/`, but canvases build on-demand (`canvas-build.ts` resolves `@maude/canvas-lib` → `canvas-lib.tsx` + sibling `.tsx` graph from disk). Fix: `stage-resources.mjs` now ships the whole `apps/studio` SOURCE tree (minus node_modules + per-platform binaries). Re-verified: `/.design/ui/Demo.tsx` → 200 `application/javascript` built from the bundled `canvas-lib.tsx`. `.dmg` 30.4 MB.

- [x] **Post-port bugfix** — `useDsTokens` fetched the tokens CSS at the **DS-root-relative** path (`/system/<ds>/colors_and_type.css`) → 404 on the main origin (which serves repo-relative); fix prepends `cfg.designRel` → `/.design/system/<ds>/colors_and_type.css` (200). Only affected the token dropdowns (caught fetch). Rebuilt (292 KB).
- [x] **User-reported "black/white screen" on CSS tab — TRUE ROOT CAUSE, FIXED (`228676c`).** After the key fix, the user STILL got the fragment (clean console, incognito, even `SPLIT=0`). Forensics via `elementFromPoint`: a bare `<span>` covered every label/header — the colour-swatch **fill span (`position:absolute; inset:0`)** anchored to `.maude` because the ported `.st-cp-swatch` had **no `position:relative`** (spec swatch was decorative-only; the old `.st-css-swatch` had it). The fill painted the selected element's **computed colour across the whole window** (dark → black screen, light → white) and the invisible color `<input>` swallowed clicks; only the box-model survived (own `position:relative`). Repro was selection-data-dependent (`computed.color` present → blanket; my synthetic injections without color → invisible overlay → looked "full"). Fix: `.st-cp-swatch { position: relative; overflow: hidden; }`. Verified with dark-colour selection: fill 16×16, clicks land, full render.
- [x] ~~User-reported "fragment" render~~ — earlier diagnosis (key collision, `6130ebb`) was real but SECONDARY; the swatch overlay above was the primary user-visible breakage.
- [x] **Prior FIXED note (`6130ebb`):** Panel rendered as just the floating box-model, no chrome. Root cause: the field inputs (box-model sides, num/text/corner/opacity) keyed `key={authored[prop] ?? ''}` → **empty key for any un-authored property** → 8 box-model siblings shared `key=""` → React "two children with the same key, ``" → children omitted. **Production react-dom (release bundle) tolerated it — which is why it rendered full in my agent-browser tests and I wrongly called it a stale/offline tab TWICE.** `react-dom.development` (the bundle a local `pnpm dev` self-heals into dist — dist was silently churned 291 KB→3.9 MB dev) OMITS the colliding children → the user-visible breakage. The user's incognito DEV console (`Encountered two children with the same key, `` … CssKnobs app.jsx:3193/3300`) was the smoking gun. Fix: `key={`${prop}:${authored[prop] ?? ## Execution Progress — Annotations FigJam v3 + AI loop — IMPLEMENTED + Waves F+G+H 2026-06-11 (uncommitted)

**Wave H (user-gate feedback round 3, 5 findings, same day):** all live-verified. (1) **Shape-kind switcher** in the ctx toolbar — model `shapeKindOf`/`convertShapeKind` converts rect⇄rounded⇄circle⇄diamond⇄triangle(-down) preserving bbox/styling/rotation/groups/ID (anchored text + binds follow); 6-button cluster, one undo record. (2) **Selection offset screen-constant** — `HALO_PAD_PX=6` single source (halo + group bbox + handle positions + cursor pad-correction all `px/zoom`). (3) **One undo per resize/rotate gesture** — store verbs `previewStroke` (transient, move-drag pattern) + `commitGesture` (single record); overlay previews per tick, commits on pointerup; live: 5-tick resize = one ⌘Z. (4) **Rotated-resize drift fixed** — `rotatedAnchorShift` compensates the center-pivot shift so the anchored corner/edge stays world-fixed (Alt skips; rect-family+image+ellipse; 5 unit tests; live NW anchor pixel-invariant). (5) **Section tool was ⇧S-only** — palette's hardcoded `DRAW_TOOLS` lacked 'section'; button added. Follow-ups: the Phase-21 corner-radius cluster (square/soft/pill) RETIRED — read as "shapes twice" next to the kind switcher (`cornerRadius` stays in model+serialization, no toolbar control); polygon toolbar parity — fill/thickness caps+setters+uniform derivations predated polygons, widened so diamond/triangle get the IDENTICAL toolbar (Stroke|Fill, swatches, thin/thick) as rect/ellipse. **Verify:** 1390/1390 (+10); tsc baseline; biome clean; dist = version-string-only diff. Methodology gotcha logged: synthetic PointerEvents flush async (space + read after timeout); CDP gestures occasionally drop — re-query + retry before diagnosing code.

**Wave G (user-gate feedback round 2, 4 findings, same day):** all addressed + live-verified (direct canvas-origin automation). (1) **Double context menu** — input-router opens the shell menu from right-button POINTERDOWN (classify button 2 → context-menu), annotation layer only claimed the later `contextmenu` event → both opened; fix = layer claims right pointerdown over a stroke at document capture (`stopImmediatePropagation` WITHOUT preventDefault so native contextmenu still follows); live: one menu. (2) **Text in diamond/triangle** — new model type `AnchorHost` (rect|ellipse|polygon); all anchor gates + `TextEditor` host widened; annotate CLI now anchors polygon labels (was standalone-below); live: hover ghost + dblclick editor + commit + byte-identical round-trip. (3) **Rotate knob → corner hover zones** — knob removed; 4 invisible 18×18 zones diagonally outside corners (screen-constant 13px reach via center→corner; custom rotate-SVG cursor; z 5 under corner handles), rotation RELATIVE to grab angle (`rotRef` — no jump), Shift 15° + magnetic cardinals kept; `resizeStroke(…, rotRef?)` + 6 unit tests; live: 46.9°→38.5° `data-rot` persisted. (4) **Chrome offset** — handles sit on the halo (bbox + 4 world px `HANDLE_PAD`, cursor pad-corrected per corner so first move doesn't grow the shape); ctx-toolbar margin 8→28 px (was covering the top connection dot). **Verify:** 1380/1380 (+8; one flaky collab-stress on first run, clean ×2 re-runs); tsc baseline; biome clean; dist diff vs HEAD = version string only (Wave G ships in source — canvas bundle builds per-request). Gotcha logged: a freshly-booted dev server can serve a STALE dev `dist/comment-mount.js` — irrelevant for annotation code (lives in the per-request canvas bundle), don't chase it.

**Wave F (user-gate feedback, 12 findings, same day):** all addressed + LIVE-verified — discovery: **agent-browser CAN drive the canvas page directly on the canvas origin** (OOPIF blocks only iframe-embedded automation; new standing verification pattern). Root-cause fix: `use-annotation-selection` containsRef was a per-render object literal → the stable `contains()` closure read the FIRST render's empty selection forever (silently broke hull/multi-drag + click-keeps-selection since Phase 5.1 — the old "code-verified only" caveat was exactly this); one-line useRef fix. New in Wave F: right-click-select + annotation context menu (copy/paste/dup/z-order/group); n/e/s/w edge resize handles; dimension-match quotas + live `W × H` label (dragged-axes-only, ⌘ suppresses); **rotation** (bottom knob — top collided with ctx toolbar, magnetic cardinals, Shift 15°, rotated render/halo/handles/hit-test, local-frame resize, `data-rot`+transform); **connector overhaul** (connection dots offset outside the edge → drag-to-connect bound curved connectors; AUTO-ROUTING: non-pinned binds re-pick the facing side on recompute — live-verified re-route both ways; explicit re-anchor = `pinned` 4th bind token; exit-normal cubic curves + sleeker heads on bound arrows — legacy bytes frozen, fixture regenerated); hover "Add text" + Enter-to-edit; edit-mode TEXT toolbar driving the EDITOR via `maude:editor-format` events (+state echo w/ request-response; I-beam verified via computed style); **section tool** (⇧S — click-through interior, border/chip select, containment drag carries content, back-of-z, dbl-click rename, reader + annotate `create.type:"section"`); rounded default shape kind. **Verify:** 1372/1372; tsc baseline; biome clean; release bundles rebuilt (comment-mount +141 B — section in Tool union). - **#7 File-tree badge/delete overlap:** open-comments badge shifts left of the delete button on hover.
- **#4 (DEEP, OPEN):** comment/select on a component SHARED across artboards always lands on the FIRST artboard — root cause: pins resolve via `document.querySelector(c.selector)` (inspect.ts:284/312/371), first-match across all artboards; the `data-cd-id` isn't artboard-scoped. Real fix = capture the artboard id at select/comment time + scope resolution to that artboard (schema + multi-file change in the untrusted bundle). Needs a focused slice.
- **#6 (DEEP) — FIXED:** multi-selected annotations couldn't be group-dragged — grabbing empty hull space fell to the marquee branch + deselected. Added a hull hit-test in `annotations-layer.tsx` onDown (union bbox of selected strokes → group drag, FigJam parity).

### Follow-up round 5 — within-artboard duplicate identity (index)
- **Root cause beyond #4:** artboard-scoping fixes ACROSS artboards, but a component repeated WITHIN one artboard (list row, reusable used twice) shares the data-cd-id → `querySelector` still grabs the first. The selector alone can never be unique there.
- **Fix:** added an **occurrence `index`** to `Selection`/`Comment`/`SelectedElement` — `selectorIndex(doc, selector, el)` captures which `querySelectorAll(selector)` match the element is; resolvers use `querySelectorAll(selector)[index]`. Plumbed through every capture (hoverTargetToSelection, canvas-shell postCompose, marquee, keyboard-select) + every resolver (resolveSelectionEl, comments-overlay screenRectFor, inspect.ts injected pin script) + the comment store (api.ts) + the app.jsx submit relay + the inspect.ts `_active.json` shape. Verified earlier that scoped selectors resolve live; index extension is build+biome+smoke clean (88/88). Could not drive the exact within-artboard duplicate click via automation (narrow cross-origin viewport) — needs user re-test.
- **Also:** migrated existing `.design/_comments/*.json` unscoped selectors → artboard-scoped (5/6, via dom_path). "shadcn"→"AI handoff" card; avatar 18→22px.

### Follow-up round 4 — done
- **#4 (DEEP) — REOPENED then FIXED PROPERLY:** the first fix patched only `dom-selection.ts hoverTargetToSelection` — but ~7 OTHER sites still built/resolved unscoped `[data-cd-id]` (canvas-shell select-halo + group-bbox + postCompose, contextual-toolbar ×2, equal-spacing, marquee, keyboard-select), and the RESOLVERS preferred `sel.id` over the scoped `sel.selector`. Added shared `scopedCdSelector()` + `resolveSelectionEl()` in dom-selection.ts and routed ALL 8 sites through them. Now every halo/pin/toolbar/spacing resolves to the artboard instance actually clicked.
- **shadcn → "AI handoff"** export card (label + "production drop"); **presence avatar** bumped 18→22px + centered initials. Smoke 88/88; release rebuilt.

Replaced with a `useCanvasChromeTheme()` hook reading `data-maude-theme` → new text on the default dark canvas now serializes `fill="#ededed"` (light, visible). (b) **highlighter cursor** now reuses the Kenney `drawing_pencil` (PEN) glyph instead of a bespoke marker — one unified Kenney library. - **Cursor revamp (user steer 2026-06-04, round 2+3):** swapped Kenney glyphs (Cursor Pack 1.1 Outline, CC0) — `move`→`pointer_b` (plain, NOT shaded), `comment`→`message_round`, `pen`→`drawing_pen`, `highlighter`→`drawing_pencil` (distinct glyphs). Pen + highlighter are **vertically mirrored** (`FLIP_V = translate(0,32) scale(1,-1)`) so they lie along the top-right→bottom-left diagonal with the nib/lead at bottom-LEFT (fixes "kreslí pod koncem, ne na špičce"); hotspots at the tip (6,18). `kenney()` takes an optional `transform` string. Hotspots verified by rendering each glyph at 10× with a hotspot dot (all tips align). canvas-cursors.test 8/8; dev-server 1250/1250; biome exit 0; tsc 3 DDR-026 baseline only; dist reverted.
- **Text-editing unification (user steer 2026-06-04, `/flow:validate` focus):** added inline **formatting keyboard shortcuts** (Cmd/Ctrl + **B/I/U**) to ALL three editors via a shared `useEditorFormat` hook — they preventDefault the browser's native execCommand (which injected markup the model couldn't read → the shortcuts were previously a no-op), preview live, and the toggled bold/italic/underline (+ preserved strike) commit onto the stroke. Threaded an `EditorFmt` through `commitEditing` + all four writers (`commitText`/`commitStickyText`/`commitStandaloneText`/`createStandaloneText`) with `normFmt`/`fmtEqual` (identity short-circuit now also checks formatting). Unified the commit triggers: sticky gained Cmd+Enter commit + an Esc-suppress-blur guard (matching standalone/anchored). **Live-verified across all 3:** text-tool Cmd+B+I → bold+italic (live + persisted); sticky Cmd+B → bold; rect-anchored text Cmd+U → underline; Enter inserts a newline in all three. Test: +1 all-four-formats combined round-trip. dev-server **1251/1251**; CLI **exit 0**; biome exit 0; tsc 3 DDR-026 baseline only; dist reverted.

### UI add-on (per user request 2026-06-03) — create a blank brief board directly from the browser

User asked: "I thought I could create a new canvas directly in the UI?" — confirmed the dev-server had no create affordance (canvas creation only via `/design:new`). Built the UI path (chose option B = extend now; new server write-surface → own security pass).
- **Server:** `POST /_api/canvas` (`api.createCanvas` + `canvas-create.ts` pure helpers; http.ts route). MAIN-ORIGIN-ONLY — deliberately absent from `startCanvasServer`'s allowlist (DDR-054); the untrusted canvas iframe 403s it (asserted in `canvas-origin-gate.test.ts`). Security boundary = one strict name allowlist `/^[\p{L}\p{N}][\p{L}\p{N} _-]{0,59}$/u` (path + JSX + JSON injection) + group allowlist + designRoot containment + 409. Single-source template via build-time text-import (DDR-045-safe).
- **Client:** "+ board" composer in the tree header (`app.jsx` + `3-shell.css`) → inline name input → POST → reloadTree + open/activate. Minified `dist/client.bundle.js` + `styles.css` rebuilt (runtime bundles untouched).
- **Tests:** `canvas-create-api.test.ts` (32: validator matrix, happy path, 409, group allowlist, F2 traversing-config). **Live-verified** via agent-browser: "+ BOARD" → type → Enter → board on disk + in tree + active + rendered; duplicate → inline error.
- **Security pass (defender + adversarial):** endpoint PASS (0 blockers). Fixed **F2** (group containment now asserts against designRoot — a poisoned config can't escape) + **NFC** normalization (clobber-bypass). **F1 (open, tracked in DDR-085):** the *ingest* path feeds untrusted annotation text verbatim to `ux-research-agent` (WebFetch + repo Read = lethal trifecta). Applied data-framing mitigation in new.md §6b.2; the architectural close (outbound-allowlisted research context) is a **follow-up decision for the user**.
- **Verify:** dev-server suite 1144/1144; biome clean; tsc baseline-only; tree clean (reverted test-boot dist + a stray `.design` viewport-meta churn).
- **Still pending for `/flow:done`:** `canvas-brief-board` web-desktop scenario; final `/flow:validate`; decision on the F1 architectural close.

- **Key fix during execution:** the provider had to move from `mountCanvas` (comment-mount.js bundle) into `DesignCanvas` (canvas-lib bundle) — separate bundles → separate React context instances; consumer never saw a provider in another bundle (same reason the real ToolProvider lives in DesignCanvas). ### Phase 13.1 — HMR error resilience during agent editing (DDR-077) ✅ 2026-06-02

User report: during a live `/design:edit` run, an agent's half-saved file (missing import / undefined symbol / transpile error) flashed the canvas to a **white screen** (hard `location.reload()` into a broken module). Asked to prevent it — **only while an agent is connected**.

- **Gate:** reuse the Phase 8 `ai-activity` signal in `_shell.html` (parent `dgn:'ai-activity'` postMessage relay when embedded + own inspector WS frame when standalone). `agentEditing` true ⇒ error-resilient path; false ⇒ unchanged `location.reload()` (manual editing keeps the white-screen "you broke it" signal).
- **Build/import error:** `import(canvasUrl + '?v=…')` rejects before any teardown → keep current render + amber "⏸ build error — držím poslední funkční verzi" toast; next good build's import swaps in via `window.__maudeCanvasRuntime.remount`.
- **Render-time throw:** resettable `CanvasErrorBoundary` (canvas-comment-mount.tsx) holds the last good render (`OkSignal` commit-effect only advances `lastGood` on a clean render) + same toast; new attempt clears + retries.
- **Files:** `canvas-comment-mount.tsx` (runtime + boundary + remount/hold API + `buildCanvasTree`), `templates/_shell.html` (agent gate + `softReload` + module/hard gating + hide-chrome suppress), `inspect.ts` (`.dc-hmr-holding` CSS), `test/canvas-hmr-runtime.test.tsx` (happy-dom boundary hold+recover), DDR-077, changeset.
- **Live-verified** (throwaway canvas + simulated agent via `/_api/ai/start`): build-error save → held render + toast (no white flash, screenshot captured); good save → soft swap + toast cleared; `/_api/ai/end` → gate false → plain reload restored. 1085 dev-server tests green; tsc baseline-only; biome clean.
- **Accepted residuals (in DDR-077):** sub-200 ms first-edit race (covered because `/design:edit` starts ai-activity before writing); ESM `?v=` module retention over a long session; per-swap canvas-state reset (same as a full reload).
- **NOTE:** Phase 13.1 code landed AFTER the `/flow:validate` run on the Phase 13 base — re-run `/flow:validate` (or let `/done` do it) so the full gate + a security pass cover the new WS-gate / dynamic-import / runtime-global surface.

### Phase 13.2 — agent presence (DDR-078) ✅ 2026-06-02

User ask: more agents working on canvases should feel like additional connected people — distinct colors, funny names, a presence avatar in the corner. Scope confirmed via AskUserQuestion: **one-per-canvas MVP** (no ai-activity multi-author refactor) · **avatar + overlay only** (no roaming cursor) · **subtle AI marker**.

- **Identity (no flow change):** derived client-side from the existing `ai-activity` entry — `${author}:${startedAt}` → deterministic funny name ("Adjective Animal", 20×20) + color via `colorForName` (the same palette human peers use). `startedAt` is stable across heartbeats → stable per-session identity.
- **`use-agent-presence.tsx` (new):** `AgentPresenceProvider` (mounted in `DesignCanvas`, canvas-lib bundle — same cross-bundle rule as DDR-077) subscribes to `ai-activity` like `ai-banner` (postMessage relay + own WS + `GET /_api/ai` seed); `useAgentPresence()` exposes the virtual peer.
- **`participants-chrome.tsx`:** renders an `AgentAvatar` (peer chip + ✦ marker + "AI agent · <author>" popover) alongside human peers; shows even with zero human peers.
- **`canvas-lib.tsx` (DCArtboard):** tints the activity overlay with `agent.color` + shows `agent.name` ("editing — Nimble Ferret") when an agent is the editor; `artboard-activity-overlay.tsx` takes an optional `color` (overrides `--mdcc-activity`).
- **Soft-reload compose fix:** `_shell.html` now keeps `window.__maude_activity_seed__` live-updated on every `activity` message (not just `snapshot`) so the overlay survives the 13.1 soft-reload remount (which skips the snapshot re-seed).
- **Live-verified:** ai-activity → colored avatar ("Sleepy Lynx" orange / "Nimble Ferret" blue) with ✦ marker + matching-color rim + "editing — <funny name>" badge (screenshots captured); survives soft-reload; non-agent manual edit unchanged (blue rim + file label, no avatar). `use-agent-presence.test.tsx` (9 tests); 1094 dev-server tests green; tsc baseline-only; biome clean.
- **Deferred:** multi-agent on the SAME file (ai-activity multi-author); roaming agent cursor; the pre-existing presence-peer-accumulation bug (agent avatars are virtual, don't worsen it).
- **NOTE:** 13.1 + 13.2 both landed after the Phase-13 `/flow:validate` — one re-run covers all three at `/done`.

### Phase 13.3 — full-artboard scan beam ✅ 2026-06-02

User ask: make the "editing pulse" a light overlay sweeping the whole artboard top→bottom (scan/edit feel), not just a rim glow.

- **`inspect.ts`:** replaced the rim `::after` opacity-pulse with a static glow + a new `.dc-activity-scan` cover whose `::after` is a soft gradient band animated `transform: translateY(-60%→60%)` (size-independent, transform-only). Inherits `--mdcc-activity` → scans in the agent's color (DDR-078). `prefers-reduced-motion` drops the beam (static rim remains). `--mdcc-activity-scan-ms` default 2200 ms.
- **`artboard-activity-overlay.tsx`:** mounts a `<div className="dc-activity-scan">` inside the rim (which is now `overflow:hidden` so the beam clips to the rounded artboard).
- **`workflows.config.json`:** `motion.customPulses.canvasActivity` 1200 → 2200 (the ambient-loop declaration, flow:motion-rules §9).
- Refinement note in DDR-075 (visual contract); changeset line added. `artboard-activity-overlay.test.tsx` asserts the scan element + the agent-color var override (5 tests). Live-verified: beam sweeps top→bottom in the agent color (frames at 6.0 s = band at top, 7.2 s = band at bottom); non-agent = blue beam. 1095 dev-server tests green; tsc baseline-only; biome clean. Runtime + injected-CSS only — no static dist change.
- **Tuned per user feedback (several rounds):** (1) "dost na sílu" → softened to base wash + blurred beam. (2) bottom-anchored wave — but its opacity breathe was imperceptible on video. (3) **Final:** the AGENT BORDER is the primary indicator — clear `2.5px` border in the agent color + a breathing glow (`::after`, opacity 0.4↔0.85, 2.2 s); the wave is now a **light STATIC bottom wash** (10%→0) behind it; label unchanged. Key fix: `overflow:hidden` was on the rim and **clipped the outward glow** (why the border was "skoro neni vidět") — moved clipping onto the child wash so the glow shows. (Also: a backtick inside an injected-CSS comment crashed boot — the whole INSPECTOR_SCRIPT is a template literal; comments there must avoid backticks.) reduced-motion → static glow. Inherits agent color (DDR-078).
- **Wave — final form (after ~5 iterations):** static 10% wash → imperceptible; opacity-breathe → imperceptible; in-place tide → "just bounces"; repeating-gradient flow → "looks like a beam again + starts mid-height". **Final:** ONE gradient — `.dc-activity-scan::after`, `linear-gradient(to top, --mdcc-activity 20% → transparent)` (sharp bottom edge, fade up), `height:135%` (taller-than-artboard so the wave is stretched + fuller per user "udělej ji vyšší"), animated `translateY(-100%→100%)` `linear` (~3.8 s). The sharp edge enters at the top, the whole wave sweeps straight down and off past the bottom, next enters from the top, loop. reduced-motion → `.dc-activity-scan { display:none }` (border's static glow remains).

RCA (`.ai/logs/rca/issue-acp-chat-cross-restart-session-resume.md`): the transcript (`_chat/<id>.jsonl`) is disk-persisted and made the panel LOOK unchanged, but `AcpBridge.sessions` (chatId→ACP sessionId) was purely in-memory per bridge instance (one per WebSocket = one per app lifetime), so every restart called `newSession()` unconditionally — a known, already-documented gap (DDR-125: "cross-restart claude memory is NOT yet wired... tracked follow-up"). **Fix (DDR-146):** persist each chat's sessionId to `_chat/<id>.session.json` (DDR-115 per-machine runtime state, already covered by the existing `_chat/` ignore in all three lists); `sessionFor()` now tries the adapter's `loadSession()` before falling back to `newSession()`; a `replaying` flag suppresses the adapter's history-replay notifications (`claude-agent-acp`'s `replaySessionHistory`) so resuming doesn't double-render/double-transcript. **Gate:** biome clean · 0 new tsc errors (touched files) · full `apps/studio` suite **1853 pass/1 skip/0 fail** (+ new resume/race/malformed-id tests) · CLI 163/163. No scenario/a11y run — backend session-lifecycle fix with no UI diff (native-only feature, DDR-123). **Security fan-out: defender PASS (0 blockers, 2 low notes); adversarial NEEDS-FIXES → fixed before close-out** — HIGH: `loadSession` could hang forever on a dead transport, wedging `replaying=true` and silently black-holing a bridge (fixed: 15s `withTimeout`, mirrors the existing `git/service.ts` pattern); MEDIUM-HIGH: concurrent `warm`+`prompt` calls for one chat raced the shared `replaying` flag (fixed: per-chatId in-flight-promise cache, mirrors `ensureStarted`'s `this.starting`); MEDIUM: unvalidated persisted `sessionId` reaching the wire (fixed: UUID-shape regex gate). Changeset (patch) + What's New entry (pending version) + site mirror regenerated. Commit `cb827e8` on `main`, **not pushed**. |

Bash-1:1 port of `/flow:init` Step 2 PLUS a workspace deep-scan extension (one level into `packages/*`, `plugins/*`, `site`, `scripts`, `examples`) so monorepos where every workspace is TS still report `language: typescript`. Detector returns `'unknown'` strings / `null` for unresolved — never throws, never fabricates. Quality detector emits package-manager-prefixed commands (e.g. `pnpm biome check .`, `npx tsc --noEmit`).

Exports `lintConfig({ config, schema })` AND `lintConfig({ configPath, schemaPath })`. Maps Ajv errors to `{ path, message, value, allowed?, suggestion? }`. Inlined Levenshtein (~15 LOC) — no extra dep.
- ✅ A9 — `cli/lib/config-lint.test.mjs` — 10 cases. Covers valid config, unknown property (additionalProperties:false), type mismatch, missing required, quality non-string, quality empty string, quality arbitrary gate names, enum suggestion (`conventionall` → `conventional`).

NO `GateSpec` object shape, no `order`, no per-gate metadata per Design Decisions § "Out of scope". Also added `$schema` as a known top-level property (editor-support pattern was breaking otherwise-valid configs).
- ✅ A11 — `cli/commands/doctor.mjs` — unified `maude doctor` CLI. Sections: Dependencies (per plugin), Config schema, Stack drift, Quality additions, Summary. Flags: `--plugin <name>` (scopes deps; config stays global), `--fix` (per-dep prompt for autoInstall + silent stack drift apply + silent additive quality merge + silent unknown-property drop; NEVER touches invalid-enum errors — those are user decisions), `--json` (structured envelope). ## Execution Progress — phase-20-canvas-undo-redo (2026-05-26)

- ✅ T1 — `undo-stack.ts` pure reducer + types + selectors. Ring-cap 50, branch-discard on push. 9 bun tests.
- ✅ T2 — `use-undo-stack.tsx` React Context Provider; ref-as-authoritative-store so async runner sees its own writes synchronously between Cmd+Z key-repeats. SSR-capture pattern + serialized inFlight promise chain. 7 contract tests.
- ✅ T3 — `input-router.tsx` `classify()` extended with `{ kind: 'undo' | 'redo' }`. Cmd+Z / Cmd+Shift+Z / Ctrl+Y / Cmd+Y. Alt-modifier guard (Cmd+Opt+Z stays browser-native). 9 new classify tests.
- ✅ T4 — `RouterCallbacks.onUndo / onRedo` + preventDefault dispatch.
- ✅ T5 — `commands/move-artboards-command.ts` — full-snapshot before/after (matches PATCH `/_api/canvas-meta` shallow-merge shape, see DDR-027). Deep-cloned + label override. `diffLayoutPositions` helper for no-op skip. 11 tests.
- ✅ T6 — `canvas-lib.tsx` `commitArtboardPositions` routes through `undoStack.push(createMoveArtboardsCommand(...))`. `UndoStackProvider` mounted at `DesignCanvas` (one level above `DesignCanvasInner`) so the commit path + the shell tree share one per-iframe context. No-op skip via `diffLayoutPositions`.
- ✅ T7 — Marquee batch drag already routes through `useArtboardDrag` → `dragBus.commitPositions` → T6 path. Single command per gesture (leader + followers in one snapshot pair).
- ✅ T8 — `commands/equal-spacing-command.ts` — label helpers only (`equalSpacingLabel` / `alignLabel`). `DragStateBus.commitPositions` extended with `opts.label`. Distribute + align now read as `Undo: equal-space N artboards` / `Undo: align left N artboards`.
- ✅ T9 — `commands/annotation-strokes-command.ts` + layer wiring. `commitStrokes(prev, next, label?)` helper cancels pending debounce timer THEN pushes command (DDR-050 gotcha). Per-stroke granularity (no coalesce window — matches Figma). All 4 inline `setStrokesState` mutator sites (setStrokes, updateStroke, deleteStrokes, translateStrokes, eraseAt, endStroke, commitText) now route through commitStrokes. 7 tests.
- ⊘ T10 — Comments **deferred per plan recommendation**. Server has `commentsDelete` API but full undo for `commentsPatch` needs CRDT-shaped author/timestamp reconciliation. Follow-up issue.
- ✅ T11 — `undo-hud.tsx` minimal top-right aria-live="polite" toast. 1.2 s auto-dismiss; `pointer-events: none`; `--dur-fast` / `--dur-base` fade tokens; `prefers-reduced-motion` 1 ms collapse.
- ✅ T12 — `canvas-shell.tsx` `<UndoHud />` mounted in `CanvasRouter` render tree; `useInputRouter` callbacks wire `onUndo`/`onRedo` to `undoStack.undo()`/`.redo()`. Provider scope decision (per DDR-050): mounted at `DesignCanvas` (T6) instead of inside CanvasShell — eliminates the postMessage forwarding path the plan considered, since input-router listens on `document` inside the iframe.
- ✅ T13 — External-edit invalidation. `patchCanvasMeta` stamps `window.__maude_last_meta_self_write_at = Date.now()` before fetch. `client/hmr.mjs` forwards `fs:json` events for `*.meta.json` matching `data-canvas-path` into iframes as `CustomEvent('maude:invalidate-undo')`. Provider listens, dampens within 500 ms self-echo window, clears stack with `"Edit history reset (external change)"` label otherwise.
- ✅ T14 — `.ai/decisions/DDR-050-canvas-undo-redo-command-stack.md` written. 7 rules: command-pattern, per-iframe scope, viewport+selection NOT undoable, Phase-8 Y.UndoManager interface freeze, depth-cap 50, external-edit clear (not merge), comments out of v0.

Anchored above the union bbox (flip-below when top < 60 px), same chrome idiom as `MultiArtboardToolbar`. Actions: Copy CSS (selector), Copy ID (data-cd-id), Comment (postMessage `dgn: 'comment-compose'`). Auto-hides when only artboards or only annotations are selected (those have their own toolbars). ## Execution Progress — canvas-figjam-feel (Wave 3.5 post-review fixes, 2026-05-26)

User-side review of Wave 3 surfaced 7 grievances; all addressed:

- ✅ **G1** — Click-on-empty deselect re-enabled. Reverts Wave-2.7 fix #1: a bare click on empty world (annotations marquee path) now clears annotation selection; bare click on empty world outside any artboard (artboard-marquee path) clears element/artboard selection. Shift-click preserves selection for additive workflows. Esc remains as the secondary deselect gesture.
- ✅ **G2** — Dropped dblclick-on-artboard auto-zoom. T33's artboard zoom-to was too magnetic and conflicted with native dblclick text-select inside chrome. Kept dblclick-on-empty → `controller.fit()` (discoverable "back to overview"); artboard zoom remains reachable via Cmd+1 + zoom HUD.
- ✅ **G3** — ContextualToolbar Comment button now opens the composer. Old code only posted `dgn: 'comment-compose'` to the parent; the actual composer listens on the iframe-level `cm:open-composer` CustomEvent. Toolbar now dispatches that event with cursor coords derived from the selected element's bbox (right edge, vertical midpoint), so the composer lands beside the element rather than at viewport origin.
- ✅ **G4** — Comment composer position. `computeAnchor` in `comments-overlay.tsx` now prefers the cursor click point (composer drops 8 px below the cursor) over the element bottom-left anchor. The element-rect fallback survives for entry points without a cursor.
- ✅ **G5** — Annotation marquee no longer fires during artboard drag. The annotation-layer pointerdown handler now bails when pointerdown lands inside any `[data-dc-screen]` artboard without hitting an actual stroke — the artboard drag / element-marquee already own that gesture, so racing the annotation marquee against them was the source of the visual noise.
- ✅ **G6** — FigJam-style 11-color palette. Replaced the muddy 6-color stroke + 6-color fill palettes with a unified 11-color palette (black / gray / red / orange / yellow / green / teal / blue / purple / pink / white) used in both Stroke and Fill modes. Identical color identity across modes preserves the swatch row when the user flips the mode toggle.
- ✅ **G7** — Align commands. New `alignArtboards(mode)` in `canvas-shell.tsx` covers 6 modes (left / right / center-x / top / bottom / center-y). Enabled at ≥ 2 artboards (alignment well-defined). 6 new icon buttons added to `MultiArtboardToolbar` before the existing 2 distribute buttons, separated by a hairline divider. 6 new menu entries in the artboard-chrome context-menu registry as discoverability backup.

**Validation:**
- 409/409 bun tests still green.
- `bunx tsc --noEmit` clean (only the pre-existing api.ts / runtime-bundle.ts baseline errors).
- `bun run build.ts` clean.

## Execution Progress — canvas-figjam-feel (Wave 3.6 user-feedback batch, 2026-05-26)

User retested after Wave 3.5, found 4 remaining issues:

- ✅ **G2v2** — Dropped `controller.jumpTo` from artboard label button onClick. Single-click on artboard chrome no longer auto-zooms. `fit-one` context-menu entry rewired through new `focusArtboard(id)` callback (looks up rect from artboardsCtx and calls jumpTo directly).
- ✅ **G6v3** — Reverted Thick from 10 → 6 (user feedback: too thick). Thin stays at 3. Thickness controls hidden when swatchMode = 'fill' (already shipped in G6v2).
- ✅ **G3v3 + G7v3 root cause (single shared bug):** the G1 fix re-enabled "click-on-empty clears selection" via the doc-level marquee `pointerup`-without-crossed handlers. But the marquee chrome filters (`shouldIgnoreTarget` in `artboard-marquee.tsx`, `isChromeTarget` in `marquee-overlay.tsx`, `CHROME_SELECTOR` in `annotations-layer.tsx`) didn't list the floating toolbar surfaces. When the user clicked any button on `MultiArtboardToolbar` or `ContextualToolbar` or `EqualSpacingHandles`:
  1. `pointerdown` lands on the button — marquee handlers see it, target NOT recognized as chrome → enter pending state.
  2. User releases without dragging → `pointerup` fires.
  3. `s.crossed === false` → my G1 path runs `selSet.clear()` BEFORE the button's `onClick` (synchronous DOM event order, capture phase doc listener fires first).
  4. Button `onClick` then runs `distributeArtboards()` / `alignArtboards()` / `openComposerForSelection()` — closure reads the just-cleared selection → bails on `< 3` / `!primary` guard.

  **Fix:** added `.dc-multi-artboard-tb, .dc-elem-ctx-tb, .dc-cv-eq-spacing-layer` (plus the previously-missing `.cm-composer, .cm-thread, .cm-mention-popup, .cm-pin, .dc-annot-resize-handle` in annotations-layer) to all three chrome filters. Doc-level marquee bails when pointerdown lands on these surfaces, selection survives the click.

  **Live verified via agent-browser:** before fix — `pointerdown→pointerup→click` on Distribute H button leaves `haloCount: 0` (selection cleared). After fix — `haloCount: 3 → 3` (selection survives), distribute fires, artboard moves. G3 composer also renders at `(571, 79)`.

  **G1 still works:** clicking actual empty world (no artboard, no toolbar) → selSet cleared (`afterSelect: 2 → afterEmptyClick: 0` verified live).

**Bonus structural fix:** extended canvas-build mtime cache invalidation to watch the entire dev-server source tree (not just `_lib/canvas-lib.tsx`). Without this, edits to `canvas-shell.tsx`, `contextual-toolbar.tsx`, etc. don't surface to canvas iframes — the stale-bundle behaviour that masked half of G3/G7 debugging. Recursive `fs.watch` over `DEV_SERVER_ROOT`, filtered to `.tsx` (server-only `.ts` skipped — doesn't bundle into canvas), excluding `test/`, `dist/`, `client/`.

**Validation:**
- 409/409 bun tests green
- `bunx tsc --noEmit` clean (DDR-026 baseline only)
- agent-browser live tests on `localhost:4555` Canvas Viewport: G2v2 (no zoom on label click) + G3 (Comment composer renders) + G7 (selection survives toolbar button click + distribute moves artboards) + G1-regression-check (empty-world click still clears) all pass.

- `plugins/design/dev-server/canvas-shell.tsx` — G2v2 (focusArtboard callback in registry, fit-one menu rewire)
- `plugins/design/dev-server/annotations-layer.tsx` — G6v3 (thick 10→6) + chrome filter expansion
- `plugins/design/dev-server/annotations-context-toolbar.tsx` — G6v3 (thick 10→6 label + value)
- `plugins/design/dev-server/artboard-marquee.tsx` — chrome filter expansion (toolbar surfaces)
- `plugins/design/dev-server/marquee-overlay.tsx` — chrome filter expansion

  1. **No auto-clear on empty-space click.** `onSelect` no-target path, annotation-layer empty-world up-click, and `artboard-marquee` threshold-cross all stripped of their `selSet.clear()` / `annotSel.clear()` calls. Marquee with zero hits preserves existing selection. Esc remains the single deselect gesture across elements / artboards / annotations.
  2. **Direct artboard drag, no ghost.** Dropped `.dc-artboard-ghost` rendering + `.dc-dragging` opacity-0.3 fade. The `<article>` now updates its own inline `left/top` to `liveX/liveY` each frame while the drag is in flight; commit-on-settle path unchanged. Removed the `dc-dragging` halo / group-bbox hide guards added in the prior fix — they're unnecessary now that the halo follows the moving article via `getBoundingClientRect`.
  3. **Distribute floating toolbar + drop ⌘⌥H / ⌘⌥V keybinds.** New `MultiArtboardToolbar` mounted in `CanvasRouter`, anchored above the group-bbox top-center (flips below when top < 60 px from viewport edge). Renders when ≥ 2 artboards selected; Distribute H / V buttons enabled at ≥ 3 (math is undefined for 2). Floating chrome ambient shadow per DDR-046 (`0 6px 24px color-mix(--fg-0 10%, transparent)` + 8 px radius). Keybindy odstraněny per user feedback — toolbar je primární surface, kontext-menu zůstává jako discoverability backup.

- ✅ Task 24.6 (follow-up) — **Artboard marquee drag-to-lasso.** New `artboard-marquee.tsx` overlay mounted in `CanvasRouter`. Bare left-button pointerdown on empty world (no `[data-dc-screen]` ancestor, no floating chrome / overlay) in move tool starts a marquee. 4 px drag threshold suppresses click jitter (early portion of T25 Wave 3 logic landed locally to unblock T24). Bare-mode clears `selSet` up-front when threshold crosses (user sees "starting fresh"); Shift-mode preserves the set for add-to-selection. `pointermove` paints `.dc-cv-marquee` div (1 px solid accent + 8 % accent fill per DDR-046 rev 2 — solid not dashed; dashed reserved for persistent group bbox). `pointerup` intersects marquee AABB with every artboard's screen-coord `getBoundingClientRect()`; hits build `Selection` entries with `artboardId` set + `[data-dc-screen="…"]` selector. Shift → `selSet.add(hits)`, bare → `selSet.replace(hits)`. With T24.5 chrome-click selection + this marquee gesture, the distribute commands now have both single-shot (Cmd+Shift+Click) and lasso entry paths.

- ✅ Task 24.5 (follow-up) — **Artboard chrome selection gesture.** Opened `resolveHoverTarget`: clicks on artboard chrome (label, header, article root) OR empty body padding return the `<article>` element with `cdId=null` and `artboardId` populated. `hoverTargetToSelection` falls back to `[data-dc-screen="…"]` selector when there's no `cdId`. Existing SelectionHalos/GroupBbox find the article via that selector and paint around the whole frame (single-select → 2 px solid + 18 % ring + 8×8 corner ticks; multi-member → 1.5 px solid; group bbox → 1 px dashed + 6×6 corner ticks). Active-artboard ring (`aria-current="true"` → 2 px box-shadow on article) stays orthogonal to selection halo — both can fire (active = "where viewport is parked", selected = "what I'm operating on"). Drag suppression from prior patch (`dc-dragging` guard) keeps the halo + group bbox hidden during artboard drag. Multi-artboard distribute (T24) now has a real gesture path: Cmd+Shift+Click on artboard chrome accumulates `selSet` entries with `artboardId` set, and the gate `selectedArtboardCount >= 3` becomes reachable. The previous T24 menu items were UI without a way to enter the precondition — this patch closes that gap.

First run caught **3 broken specimens** with unescaped `{` / `}` chars in JSX text content (`components-code-block`, `components-diff-view`, `type-mono`). Fixed via `{'{'}` / `{'}'}` escapes + `<pre>{\`...\`}</pre>` template wrap. Re-run: **38/38 PASS**.
- DDR-022 (canvas-lib virtual module + inline-on-handoff) + DDR-023 (no html-to-jsx codemod, specimens are bare TSX) recorded.

## Phase 3.6.1 visual-regression repair (2026-05-18, post-/validate)

When the user opened the migrated specimens in the dev-server they reported widespread visual breakage. Live screenshot review confirmed three discrete failure modes; all fixed in this session:

1. **Triple-chrome above specimen content.** The migrated specimens were wrapped in `<DesignCanvas><DCSection title="..."><DCArtboard label="..." width={0} height={0}>...</DCArtboard></DCSection></DesignCanvas>`. The DCSection's `<h2>` title strip + DCArtboard's `dc-artboard-label sku` SKU strip both rendered ABOVE the original `<header class="specimen-hd">` — three header rows where the original HTML had one.

2. **`htmlFor` bleeding into prose.** The html-to-jsx codemod's attribute-rename regex (`\s([a-zA-Z-]...)(=...|(?=\s|/>|>))`) matched plain words in text content. The word "for" in sentences like "a library for the marketplace" got rewritten to `htmlFor`. Same risk class would have hit `readonly`, `disabled`, `checked`, `selected`, `hidden`, etc. — every boolean attribute name.

3. **Sibling CSS dropped.** `import "./<slug>.css"` in specimen TSX produced a separate `.css` asset via Bun.build, but `buildCanvasModule()` only took `outputs[0]` (the JS entry-point) and discarded the rest. Specimens with bespoke per-file CSS (`ui_kits-desktop-showcase`, `motion`, `state-system`, `logo`, `iconography`, ...) rendered as unstyled text dumps in the browser.

### Scope correction — user direction

User explicitly stated `html-to-jsx` is unnecessary scaffolding: specimens should be authored as native TSX with no codemod layer at all. Phase 3.6.1's "specimens migration" track is **collapsed to a one-shot manual migration** — going forward, specimens are bare TSX written by hand or by sub-agents during DS bootstrap (per the updated SKILL.md sub-agent prompt). No `migrate-canvases.ts`, no `html-to-jsx.ts`.

### Changes this session

- **DELETED:** `plugins/design/dev-server/html-to-jsx.ts`, `plugins/design/dev-server/test/html-to-jsx.test.ts`, `plugins/design/dev-server/test/migrate-specimens.test.ts`, `scripts/migrate-canvases.ts`. Tests dropped from 149 → 123 (still ahead of Phase 3.6 baseline of 95).
- **canvas-lib.tsx + template** — reverted the `width=0/height=0` auto-flow special case + `bare` DCSection prop. DCArtboard now ONLY renders fixed-px chrome (UI mocks); specimens never wrap themselves in it.
- **Specimens stripped (`.design/system/project/preview/*.tsx`)** — 38 specimens hand-fixed: dropped the `<DesignCanvas><DCSection><DCArtboard>` envelope, replaced with bare `<><header class="specimen-hd">...</header><main class="specimen">...</main></>`. Also globally replaced `htmlFor ` → `for ` in prose text (left `htmlFor=`/`htmlFor={...}` in actual attribute positions). Carried out via a one-shot Bun script (deleted after run).
- **canvas-build.ts** — `buildCanvasModule()` now collects every `kind: "asset"` CSS output from `Bun.build` and prepends a self-installing `<style data-canvas-css="bundled">` injector to the JS bundle. Idempotent per-slug; works under both module + hard HMR reloads.
- **ds-specimen.tsx.template** — rewritten to scaffold bare TSX with `specimen-hd` + `<main class="specimen">` shape. No envelope.
- **design-system/SKILL.md** — sub-agent prompt block flipped: specimens are bare TSX, NO `@maude/canvas-lib` import. UI mock canvases (Docs Site, Canvas Viewport, Smoke TSX) keep the envelope.

### Visual verification (live dev-server, agent-browser)

Started the Bun-based dev-server (`bun plugins/design/dev-server/server.ts`) — note that `bin/server-up.sh` boots the legacy `server.mjs` (zero-dep Node, no TSX pipeline). The Bun server is what wires the canvas-build pipeline + CSS injector.

Captured screenshots of 38 specimens + 3 UI canvases. Sampled and confirmed visually correct:

- ✓ colors-accent · type-scale · components-buttons · components-toggles · iconography · logo · motion · borders · empty-state · components-cards · ui_kits-desktop-showcase — all render with full styling, single specimen-hd, original layout intact.
- ✓ Docs Site (5 stacked artboards at 1440×900) · Canvas Viewport — UI canvas chrome (`dc-canvas` / `dc-section` / `dc-artboard` + label strips) renders correctly via their sibling CSS.
- ✓ Smoke TSX renders functionally (counter + h1 + bare button); intentionally has no chrome styling (it's a foundation smoke fixture).

### Known carry-over

- `bin/server-up.sh` still launches `server.mjs` (legacy Node server, no TSX pipeline). The maude design serve story for the Bun-based server is a separate follow-up — currently you boot it manually via `bun plugins/design/dev-server/server.ts --root . --port 4399`. Not blocking Phase 3.6.1 close-out but worth a Phase 3.6.2 or DDR.
- Tests: 123 pass / 0 fail (down from 149 only because the codemod tests went with the codemod). tsc clean except for the two pre-existing api.ts errors.

## Phase 3.6 close-out note (2026-05-18)

Phase 3.6 (canvas TSX format) **shipped** with all 12 tasks marked complete + 95 tests green + handoff CLI end-to-end-verified. Acceptance criteria as written were met. Runtime hygiene gaps surfaced when the user opened the migrated canvases in the dev-server:

- `Docs Site.tsx` + `Canvas Viewport.tsx` white-page at runtime — codemod produced JSX that referenced `<DesignCanvas>`/`<DCSection>`/`<DCArtboard>` but didn't define them (originals relied on babel-runtime window globals). Build + tests pass; runtime fails.
- `Smoke TSX.tsx` lacks the canvas envelope (was a foundation-slice mount fixture, never upgraded).
- DS specimens left as `.html` (Plan Task 9 explicitly skip-listed them) breaks the plug-and-play promise — inspector select + `/design:edit` don't work on specimens.
- HMR was never wired (carries from runtime slice).

3.6 acceptance criteria didn't require "renders without console errors" — only "transpile + build". That gap caused the disconnect. 3.6.1 plan adds that gate + introduces a project-owned `@maude/canvas-lib` shared library (resolved virtually, inlined on handoff) + wires HMR + flips DS specimens to TSX.

Closing 3.6 as **shipped + documented**; 3.6.1 is the follow-up.

Emits `<Slug>.registry.json` per [shadcn registry-item schema](https://ui.shadcn.com/schema/registry-item.json). `stripDataCdId()` AST-removes the pipeline scaffolding from the dropped TSX. `classifyImports()` separates npm specs from `@/components/ui/*` registry deps via `Bun.Transpiler.scanImports()`. React.useEffect ⇒ bare `useEffect` import rewrite handled.

Skips cleanly on fresh checkouts via `existsSync` guard. Real CLI run produces `Docs Site.registry.json`: 57 KB, 3 files (component 45 KB no `data-cd-id`, style 3 KB subset of `_components.css`, theme 2 KB of touched tokens), 30 cssVars surfaced.

- ✅ Task 12: AI-handoff polish.
  - 12a — `canvas-header.ts` JSDoc projector module. Idempotent block-comment overwrite via `applyHeaderToSource()`; surfaces `ai_context.why_this_exists` → `@notes`, `pinned_decisions[]` → `@decision`, `known_quirks[]` → `@quirk`. CLI entry for `/design:edit` to shell out. 8 new tests in `test/canvas-header.test.ts`. JSDoc generation is also baked into Task 8's codemod (every migrated canvas already ships a header).
  - 12b — `_components.css` + token bundling in `handoff.ts`. AST-scan canvas TSX for every `className` literal (covers string concats, template-literal quasis, ternaries via generic-recurse). `filterComponentsCss()` keeps rules whose first class is in the harvested set, including BEM-modifier derivatives (`.btn--ghost` rides along when `btn` is referenced). `filterTokensCss()` plucks only the `var(--*)` references the kept CSS touches. Emitted as `files[1]: registry:style` + `files[2]: registry:theme` + `cssVars.theme`.
  - 12c — `/design:edit` Step 1.5 added. Auto-loads `_components.css` + `colors_and_type.css` into orchestrator context BEFORE dispatching to frontend-design, gated on `css_mode === "inline"` + (style-verb-in-feedback OR `selected.v === 2`). Bounded cost (~6 KB CSS context) vs. the unbounded "Claude re-grep'd mid-edit" round-trip.
  - 12d — `ai_context` schema field (combined with Task 10).

## Execution Progress — phase-3.6-canvas-tsx-format (runtime slice, 2026-05-18)

Tasks 4–6 landed in second session of 2026-05-18 — TSX canvases now browser-loadable end-to-end (page mounts, useState round-trips, inspector reads data-cd-id).

- ✅ Task 4: `inspect.ts` upgraded — `SelectedElement.v: 1 | 2` schema (v=2 when click-target has an ancestor `data-cd-id`; v=1 fallback for legacy .html + shell chrome clicks). INSPECTOR_SCRIPT.elInfo() adds `id` via `closest('[data-cd-id]')`. Server-side `setSelected()` derives canvas slug from path. 3 new tests in `test/active-state.test.ts` (v=2 round-trip, v=1 fallback, slug derivation).

- ✅ Task 5: `canvas-edit.ts` + `bin/canvas-edit.sh` + `/design:edit` Step 3a. AST-aware single-attribute edits via oxc-parser + magic-string. Supports `className` swap/insert, `style.<prop>` swap/insert in inline ObjectExpression, plain string attrs (aria-label, etc.). Per-canvas mutex + atomic-rename write. Refuses to edit `data-cd-id` (pipeline-owned). CLI entry `bun canvas-edit.ts --invoke <canvas> <id> <attr> <value>` for `/design:edit` to shell out. 11 new tests in `test/canvas-edit.test.ts`. `edit.md` Step 3a documents the fast-path triggers (active = .tsx + selected.v=2 + single-element single-attribute feedback) and what it skips (steps 5 + 6 + 3.5 element-focused screenshot).

- ✅ Task 6: TSX canvases browser-loadable end-to-end. Five sub-deliverables:
  - `runtime-bundle.ts` — pre-built React 19 + ReactDOM + jsx-runtime bundles served at `/_canvas-runtime/<pkg>.js`. Per-package sub-bundles + cross-bundle externals (each bundle externalises the others; importmap stitches at browser-level → singleton React preserved). Dynamic export discovery via `await import(pkg)` enumerates ALL keys including `__CLIENT_INTERNALS_DO_NOT_USE_OR_WARN_USERS_THEY_CANNOT_UPGRADE` (required for ReactDOM to find React's shared internals — without it: `Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'S')` on first createRoot). **NODE_ENV=production** baked in — the dev React variant trips a Bun.build CJS-rename collision (`Assignment to constant variable` on `import * as React`); production variant is collision-free + smaller. 6 tests in `test/runtime-bundle.test.ts`.
  - `canvas-build.ts` — wraps `canvas-pipeline.ts` (pass-1 data-cd-id injection) with a Bun.build pass that produces browser-loadable ESM. Virtual-loader plugin feeds the with-IDs source to Bun.build (filter uses suffix-match because macOS /tmp → /private/tmp symlinks defeat exact-path matching). React + jsx-runtime + ReactDOM externalised. 5 tests in `test/canvas-build.test.ts`.
  - `http.ts` — new routes `/_canvas-runtime/<slug>.js` (lazy-built, etag-cached, 304-aware) + `/_canvas-shell.html` (serves the static shell template). TSX-canvas route swapped from `transpileCanvasSource` direct → `buildCanvasModule` (the route's `js` body is now browser-loadable, not just parseable). Existing `canvas-route.test.ts` updated for Bun.build's `export { X as default }` form.
  - `plugins/design/templates/_shell.html` — shared canvas mount harness with importmap (`react`/`react-dom`/`react-dom/client`/`react/jsx-runtime`/`react/jsx-dev-runtime` → `/_canvas-runtime/*.js`), async dynamic-import of the canvas TSX, `createRoot(root).render(<Canvas/>)`. Query params `?canvas=<rel>` + `?designRel=<rel>` + optional `?tokens=` / `?components=`.
  - `plugins/design/templates/canvas.tsx.template` — JSDoc header projecting `.meta.json` (@canvas/@ds/@platform/@opt_out/@artboards/@brief/@stack/@history/@handoff) + envelope with DesignCanvas + DCSection + DCArtboard primitives inlined locally (handoff-friendly — no runtime dep on dev-server chrome).
  - `client/app.jsx` — `canvasUrl(p, cfg)` helper switches `.tsx` paths to `/_canvas-shell.html?canvas=…&designRel=…` while keeping `.html` on the legacy direct-load path. App fetches `/_config` once at boot + threads `cfg` into Viewport. Existing build (`bun run build.ts`) refreshed.
  - `/design:new` step 3 + 7 + 8 patched — default target now `.tsx`, validation accepts default-export React component + no `<!doctype>`, write step references the canvas.tsx.template. Legacy `.html` path kept for backwards compat until Task 8 codemod lands.

## Execution Progress — phase-3.6-canvas-tsx-format (foundation slice, 2026-05-18)

- ✅ Task 0: DDR-019 written (`.ai/decisions/DDR-019-canvas-tsx-format.md`). Renumbered from plan's "DDR-017" because 017 + 018 were taken by Phase 3.5. DDR-019 reconciles with DDR-007: `data-cd-id` (transpiler-emitted, universal) and `data-dc-element` (author-emitted, semantic) coexist with documented inspector preference order. DDR index updated; 017 + 018 also indexed (were missing from README).
- ✅ Task 1: `plugins/design/dev-server/canvas-pipeline.ts` — two-pass transform via `oxc-parser` (parse) + `magic-string` (byte-range inject) + `Bun.Transpiler` (TSX→JS). ID = `Bun.hash(componentName + ":" + idx).toString(16).slice(0, 8)` — 8 hex chars, no `blake3-wasm` dep added (plan permitted Bun.hash). 15 bun:test cases green; covers determinism, whitespace-stability, sibling-insert contract, robustness (arrow components, JSXMemberExpression, idempotency on re-transpile of post-pass-1 source, malformed source → TranspileError).
- ✅ Task 2: `plugins/design/dev-server/locator.ts` — per-canvas `_locator.json` writer with per-path Promise-mutex + atomic rename. Top-level keyed by canvas slug (POSIX, ext-less, relative to designRoot). 14 bun:test cases green; covers roundtrip, multi-slug isolation, deterministic sorted-key JSON, 20-way concurrent multi-slug writes, 10-way concurrent same-slug last-writer-wins, clearLocatorSlug, malformed-JSON-as-empty.
- ✅ Task 3: `plugins/design/dev-server/http.ts` — TSX-route hooked into the existing fall-through `fetch()`. URL pattern `/.design/ui/<file>.tsx` (matches existing shell `urlOf()` helper that prefixes designRel into the iframe src). 200 + `application/javascript` + ETag (`Bun.hash(post-pass-1 source).toString(16)`); 304 on `If-None-Match` match; 500 + readable body on `TranspileError`; 404 / 403 on missing / traversal. In-memory `(absPath -> { mtimeMs, etag, js })` cache skips re-transpile when source unchanged. Locator is written synchronously before the response. 7 bun:test cases green.

**Deps added (workspace `plugins/design/dev-server/package.json`):**

- `oxc-parser ^0.131.0` (devDependency; ESTree-compatible TSX parser, ~1–3 ms for canvas-scale input)
- `magic-string ^0.30.21` (devDependency; byte-range edits, used by Rollup/Vite in production)
- Skipped `blake3-wasm` per plan permission — `Bun.hash` is 64-bit; sliced to 8 hex chars = 32 bits = ample for ≤300-element canvases.

**Validation status:**

- `bun test` — 44 pass / 0 fail across 10 files (the new 36 expects fold cleanly into the existing 8-file suite).
- `bun tsc --noEmit` — clean for new files. Pre-existing `api.ts(457,25)` error confirmed via stash-then-tsc — not introduced by this session.
- `bun run build.ts` — not re-run; route + pipeline run under bun source-mode. Should be re-built when Task 6 ships the runtime bundle that makes the JS browser-loadable.

**Out-of-scope / carries to next session:**

- **Browser-loadability of the route response.** `Bun.Transpiler.transformSync` output uses internal `jsxDEV_<hash>` symbol names meant for Bun's runtime — not browser-resolvable as-is. Task 6 (`_shell.html` + `/_canvas-runtime/react.bundle.js`) closes this; Tasks 0–3 stop at "valid JS by parse-check" (DDR-019 explicitly notes this — "making it BROWSER-loadable is the _shell.html + react-runtime bundle's job"). Tests verify the JS is re-parseable by `oxc-parser`; nothing more is promised this session.
- Tasks 4–12: inspector contract upgrade (data-cd-id reader in inspect.ts), `/design:edit` AST-aware element edits (canvas-edit.ts + bin/canvas-edit.sh), `_shell.html` + `canvas.tsx.template` + `/design:new` rewrite, `handoff.ts` + `/design:handoff` (shadcn registry-item.json sidecar), `scripts/migrate-canvases.ts` codemod + one-shot migration, sweep of `.html` refs across `plugins/design/commands/*` + skills, schema updates (`canvas-meta.schema.json.css_mode` + `data-cd-id-version`, `config.schema.json.handoffTargets`), e2e regression, AI-handoff polish (JSDoc headers, CSS bundling in registry-item, edit-context CSS auto-load).
- Performance budget gates from the plan (per-canvas TSX < 35 KB, two-pass < 8 ms p50, cold load < 250 ms, etc.) — not yet measured. Most are gated on Task 8 (codemod) producing real-world migrated canvases.

## Decisions

- DDR-001 Monorepo with single npm publisher (Phase 1)
- DDR-002 Release flow via Changesets, with parity-preserving wrapper (Phase 1)
- DDR-003 `/flow:release` walks user-authored runbook instead of dispatching on provider (Phase 3)
- DDR-004 Flow commands use `<group>-<verb>` prefix; compat stubs shipped in v0.6.0, removed in v0.6.1 (Phase 13)
- DDR-005 Docs site stack — Fumadocs + Vercel; accept Fumadocs DS defaults (Phase 2)
- DDR-006 Plugin commands/skills/agents declare `name: <plugin>:<slug>` in frontmatter (ad-hoc, 2026-05-13)
- DDR-007 Stable element-id schema — `data-dc-screen` + `data-dc-element` (Phase 13, 2026-05-15)
- DDR-008 `plugins/design/dev-server/bin/` is the canonical home for shared bash helpers (Phase 13, 2026-05-15)
- DDR-010 `design-system-keeper` agent — read-only DS-fidelity audit between generation and the critic panel (Phase 14, 2026-05-15) [DDR-009 was claimed by the bun-runtime DDR mid-session]
- DDR-011 Re-skin fumadocs via `--color-fd-*` overrides; do NOT fork (feature-docs-site-mdcc-skin, 2026-05-15)
- DDR-012 React 19 everywhere — shell and canvases share a single runtime (Phase 3.4, 2026-05-15)
- DDR-013 Server modular split into seven TypeScript modules on `Bun.serve` (Phase 3.4, 2026-05-15)
- DDR-014 CSS `@layer reset, tokens, layout, shell, components, utilities` + Lightning CSS at build time (Phase 3.4, 2026-05-15)
- DDR-015 Per-platform Bun binary distribution via npm `optionalDependencies` sub-packages with postinstall-hardlink (Phase 3.4 Tasks 12-13, 2026-05-15)
- DDR-016 `plugins/design/dev-server/runtime/` is the canvas-runtime library home — runtime code, not meta-design (Phase 3.4 Task 1 audit, 2026-05-15)
- DDR-017 Dev-server shell = shadcn-style menubar + single-canvas viewport (tabs row killed) — Phase 3.5 mid-session pivot from action-button header after user pushback on "chrome not mocked" exemption (2026-05-17)
- DDR-018 Tree groups via `kind` discriminator (`project | canvas | runtime`) — server scans PROJECT root + RUNTIME gitignored alongside canvases; DS canvas group widens to non-HTML scan (2026-05-17)
